\varepsilon\kappa
,
r\geqslant 2
.
Notice that the above estimates are independent of
\delta
as
\varepsilon
goes to zero firstly. The estimate

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\nabla\rho_{m}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{2}}\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C

implies

(2.76)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\rho_{m}^{\gamma}\right\|_{L_{t}^{1}L_{x}^{3}}\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

together with
\rho_{m}^{\gamma}\in L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{1}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right))
, by Hölder’s inequality we have

(2.77)

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\rho_{m}^{\gamma}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\frac{5}{3}}L_{x}^{\frac{5}{3}}}\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\rho_{m}^{\gamma}\right\|^{\frac{2}{5}r}_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{1}}\left\|\rho_{m}^{\gamma}\right\|^{\frac{3}{5}r}_{L_{t}^{1}L_{x}^{3}}\right]\leqslant C,

where
C
depends on
T,r
and
\varepsilon
,
r\geqslant 2
. For
\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}
, for some
r\geqslant 2
, we estimate

(2.78)

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{2}}\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{\infty}}^{r}\left\|\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{u}_{m}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{2}}^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

due to
H^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\hookrightarrow C^{0,\frac{1}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,
\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{u}_{m}\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right))
, where
C
depends on
T,r
and
\varepsilon\kappa
,
r\geqslant 2
.
We calculate

(2.79)

\nabla(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m})=2\nabla\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\otimes\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{u}_{m}+\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla\mathbf{u}_{m},\\

so we have

\nabla\left(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}\right)\in L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{2}\left(0,T;L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
,
W^{1,\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\hookrightarrow L^{3}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
.
\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}
are bounded in
\L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{2}\left([0,T];L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
,
r\geqslant 2
. This is the end of the proof.
\square

We choose the path space

(2.80)

\mathcal{X}_{2}=\mathcal{X}_{\rho_{0}}^{2}\times\mathcal{X}_{\mathbf{q}_{0}}^{2}\times\mathcal{X}_{\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0}}}}^{2}\times\mathcal{X}_{\rho}^{2}\times\mathcal{X}_{\mathbf{u}}^{2}\times\mathcal{X}_{\rho\mathbf{u}}^{2}\times\mathcal{X}_{W}^{2},

where

\mathcal{X}_{\rho_{0}}^{2}=L^{\gamma}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\cap L^{1}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\cap L^{-10}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\cap H^{9}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,
\mathcal{X}_{\mathbf{q}_{0}}^{2}=L^{1}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right))
,

\mathcal{X}_{\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0}}}}^{2}=L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,

\mathcal{X}_{\rho}^{2}=L^{2}\left([0,T];H^{10}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\cap L^{2}\left([0,T];W^{1,3}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\cap L^{\frac{5}{3}\gamma}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,
\mathcal{X}_{\mathbf{u}}^{2}=L^{2}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\cap L^{4}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,

\mathcal{X}_{\rho\mathbf{u}}^{2}=L^{2}\left([0,T];W^{1,\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\cap C\left([0,T];L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
,

\mathcal{X}_{W}^{2}=C\left([0,T];\mathcal{H}\right)
.

Step 2:
Show the tightness of the laws.

Proposition 2.4
.

\left\{\mathcal{L}\left[\rho_{0,m},\mathbf{q}_{0,m},\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,m}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0,m}}},\rho_{m},\mathbf{u}_{m},\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m},W_{m}\right];m\in\mathds{N}\right\}
is tight on
\mathcal{X}_{2}
.

To prove this proposition, we need to prove proposition
2.5
–
2.7
.

Proposition 2.5
.

The set
\left\{\mathcal{L}\left[\rho_{0,m}\right];m\in\mathds{N}\right\}
is tight on
\mathcal{X}_{\rho_{0}}^{2}
. The set
\left\{\mathcal{L}\left[\mathbf{q}_{0,m}\right];m\in\mathds{N}\right\}
is tight on
\mathcal{X}_{\mathbf{q}_{0}}^{2}
,
and
\left\{\mathcal{L}\left[\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,m}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0,m}}}\right];m\in\mathds{N}\right\}
is tight on
\mathcal{X}_{\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0}}}}^{2}
.

Probability measures on the
\sigma
-algebra of Borel sets of any Polish space are Radon measures. Therefore probability measures are inner regular or tight, see §
9.
in Appendix.
We recall that
L^{p}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)(1\leqslant p<+\infty)
is separable,
L^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
is inseparable, and
C\left([a,b]\right)
, space of continuous functions in
[a,b]
is separable.

L^{p}(1\leqslant p<+\infty)
is complete so that it is a Polish space. Consequently,
\mathcal{L}\left[\rho_{0,m}\right]
is tight on

\mathcal{X}_{\rho_{0}}^{2}=L^{\gamma}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
.
Similarly, we get the tightness of the measures generated by
\mathbf{q}_{0,m},\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,m}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0,m}}}
.

Proposition 2.6
.

The set
\left\{\mathcal{L}\left[\mathbf{u}_{m}\right];m\in\mathds{N}\right\}
is tight on
\mathcal{X}_{\mathbf{u}}^{2}
. The set
\left\{\mathcal{L}\left[\rho_{m}\right];m\in\mathds{N}\right\}
is tight on
\mathcal{X}_{\rho}
.

Proof.
For any given
\iota>0
, some
r\geqslant 2
,
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{m}\right\|_{L_{t}^{4}L_{x}^{4}}^{4r}\right]\leqslant C\left(r_{0}\right)
, let
L\geqslant\frac{\iota}{C}
, and we define the set

(2.81)

B_{L}=\left\{\mathbf{u}_{m}\in L^{4}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)|\left\|\mathbf{u}_{m}\right\|_{L_{t}^{4}L_{x}^{4}}\leqslant L\right\}.

Then it holds

(2.82)

\mathcal{L}\left[\mathbf{u}_{m}\right](B^{c}_{L})=\mathbb{P}\left[\left\{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{m}\right\|_{L_{t}^{4}L_{x}^{4}}\geqslant L\right\}\right]\leqslant\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{m}\right\|_{L_{t}^{4}L_{x}^{4}}^{4r}\right]}{L^{4r}}\leqslant\frac{C}{L^{4r}}\leqslant\iota,

which shows the tightness of
\left\{\mathcal{L}\left[\mathbf{u}_{m}\right];m\in\mathds{N}\right\}
.
For the more information about
\rho_{m}
, we calculate that

(2.83)

\triangle\rho_{m}=2\left|\nabla\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right|^{2}+2\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\triangle\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}.\\

Since
\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\in L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{\infty}\left(0,T;H^{1}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
,
H^{1}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\hookrightarrow L^{6}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,

\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\in L^{r}(\Omega;L^{2}(0,T;H^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)))
, it holds

\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\triangle\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\in L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{2}\left(0,T;L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
for some
r\geqslant 2
.

\left|\nabla\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right|^{2}\in L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{2}\left(0,T;L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
holds for some
r\geqslant 2
similarly. This proves the proposition.
\square

Proposition 2.7
.

The set
\left\{\mathcal{L}\left[\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}\right];m\in\mathds{N}\right\}
is tight on
\mathcal{X}_{\rho\mathbf{u}}^{2}
.

Proof.
We first consider the deterministic part, and we denote

\displaystyle Y_{m}(t)=

\displaystyle\Pi_{m}\left(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(0)\right)-\int_{0}^{t}\Pi_{m}\left(\operatorname{div}(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}\otimes\mathbf{u}_{m})\right)\operatorname{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}\Pi_{m}\left(\operatorname{div}(\rho_{m}\mathbb{D}\mathbf{u}_{m})\right)\operatorname{d}s

(2.84)

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\Pi_{m}\left(\nabla\rho_{m}^{\gamma}\right)\operatorname{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}\frac{11}{10}\eta\Pi_{m}\left(\nabla\rho_{m}^{-10}\right)\operatorname{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}\delta\Pi_{m}\left(\rho_{m}\nabla\triangle^{9}\rho_{m}\right)\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}r_{0}\Pi_{m}\left(|\mathbf{u}_{m}|^{2}\mathbf{u}_{m}\right)\operatorname{d}s-\int_{0}^{t}r_{1}\Pi_{m}\left(\rho_{m}|\mathbf{u}_{m}|^{2}\mathbf{u}_{m}\right)\operatorname{d}s-\int_{0}^{t}r_{2}\Pi_{m}\left(\mathbf{u}_{m}\right)\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\left(\varepsilon\Pi_{m}\left(\triangle^{2}\mathbf{u}_{m}\right)+\varepsilon\Pi_{m}\left(\nabla\rho_{m}\nabla\mathbf{u}_{m}\right)\right)\operatorname{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\Pi_{m}\left(\rho_{m}\left(\nabla\left(\frac{\triangle\sqrt{\rho_{m}}}{\sqrt{\rho_{m}}}\right)\right)\right)\operatorname{d}s.

We claim that
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|Y_{m}(t)\right\|_{C^{0,\varsigma}([0,T];W^{-l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right))}^{r}\right]\leqslant C(T,r)
for some
r>4
, where
\varsigma<\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{r}
,
l>\frac{5}{2}
.
In fact,
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\rho_{m}\right\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{\gamma}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right))}^{r}\right]\leqslant C(T,r)
,

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{4}}\mathbf{u}_{m}\right\|_{L^{4}([0,T];L^{4}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right))}^{r}\right]\leqslant C(T,r,r_{1})
,
rewrite
\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}\otimes\mathbf{u}_{m}=\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{u}_{m}\otimes\mathbf{u}_{m}
,
so
\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}\otimes\mathbf{u}_{m}\in L^{2}\left([0,T];L^{\frac{2\gamma}{\gamma+1}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
,

\left\{\Pi_{m}\left(\operatorname{div}\left(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}\otimes\mathbf{u}_{m}\right)\right)\right\}
is bounded in
L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{2}\left([0,T];W^{-1,\frac{2\gamma}{\gamma+1}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
, which is compactly embedded in
W^{-l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
, for
l>\frac{5}{2}
.
Similarly, by (
2.57
), (
2.60
), (
2.2.2
) with
R\rightarrow\infty
, and Sobolev’s embedding, we know that the families
\left\{\nabla p(\rho_{m})\right\}
,
\left\{\Pi_{m}\left(\operatorname{div}(\rho_{m}\mathbb{D}\mathbf{u}_{m})\right)\right\}
,
\left\{\Pi_{m}\left(\frac{11}{10}\eta\nabla\rho_{m}^{-10}\right)\right\}
,
\left\{\Pi_{m}\left(\varepsilon\nabla\rho_{m}\nabla\mathbf{u}_{m}\right)\right\}
,

\left\{\Pi_{m}\left(\kappa\operatorname{div}\left(\rho_{m}\nabla^{2}\ln\rho_{m}\right)\right)\right\}
, and

\left\{\Pi_{m}\left(\delta\rho_{m}\nabla\triangle^{9}\rho_{m}\right)\right\}
are bounded in
L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{2}\left([0,T];W^{-l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
, for
l>\frac{5}{2}
.
Therefore
\frac{\operatorname{d}}{\operatorname{d}t}Y_{m}(t)
is bounded
in
L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{\frac{4}{3}}\left([0,T];W^{-l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
, for
l>\frac{5}{2}
.
For any test function
\varphi(x)\in W^{l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,
\left\|\varphi\right\|_{W^{l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}=1
, by stochastic Fubini’s theorem, we estimate

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|Y_{m}(t_{2})\right\|_{W^{-l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}-\left\|Y_{m}(t_{1})\right\|_{W^{-l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|Y_{m}(t_{2})-Y_{m}(t_{1})\right\|_{W^{-l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\right)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sup\limits_{\varphi}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\operatorname{d}Y_{m}(t)\varphi(x)\operatorname{d}x\right)^{r}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sup\limits_{\varphi}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\varphi(x)\operatorname{d}Y_{m}(t)\operatorname{d}x\right)^{r}\right]

(2.85)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sup\limits_{\varphi}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\varphi(x)\frac{\operatorname{d}}{\operatorname{d}t}Y_{m}(t)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t\right)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sup\limits_{\varphi}\left(\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\left(\left\|\varphi(x)\right\|_{W^{l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\right)^{4}\operatorname{d}t\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\left\|\frac{\operatorname{d}}{\operatorname{d}t}Y_{m}(t)\operatorname{d}x\right\|_{W^{-l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}^{\frac{4}{3}}\operatorname{d}t\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}\right)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant C(t_{2}-t_{1})^{\frac{r}{4}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup\limits_{\varphi}\left(\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\left\|\frac{\operatorname{d}}{\operatorname{d}t}Y_{m}(t)\operatorname{d}x\right\|_{W^{-l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}^{\frac{4}{3}}\operatorname{d}t\right)^{\frac{3r}{4}}\right]\leqslant C(t_{2}-t_{1})^{\frac{r}{4}},

by Kolmogorov-Centov’s theorem, it holds
\left\|\bar{Y}_{m}(t)\right\|_{C^{0,\varsigma}([0,T];W^{-l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right))}\leqslant C
uniformly in
m
, where
\bar{Y}_{m}(t)
is a modification of
Y_{m}(t)
, and
C
depends on
T
and
r
,
\bar{\mathbb{P}}
a.s., for
\varsigma<\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{r}
. We will use the notation
Y_{m}(t)
here and hereafter.
Next, we consider the time regularity of stochastic integral. Applying Burkhölder-Davis-Gundy’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\rho_{m}\Pi_{m}(\mathbb{F}(\rho_{m},\mathbf{u}_{m}))\operatorname{d}W_{m}\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\right)^{r}\right]

(2.86)

\displaystyle\leqslant C\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\left|\rho_{m}\Pi_{m}(\mathbb{F}(\rho_{m},\mathbf{u}_{m}))\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}t\operatorname{d}x\right)^{\frac{r}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant C\sum\limits_{k=1}^{\infty}f_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\left|\rho_{m}\right|^{2}+\left|\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}\right|^{2}\right)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t\right)^{\frac{r}{2}}\right],

where

(2.87)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\rho_{m}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t\right)^{\frac{r}{2}}\right]\leqslant

\displaystyle C(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1})^{\frac{r}{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\rho_{m}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{3}}\right)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1})^{\frac{r}{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H_{x}^{1}}^{2}\right)^{r}\right],

(2.88)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t\right)^{\frac{r}{2}}\right]\leqslant

\displaystyle C(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1})^{\frac{r}{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\rho_{m}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{3}}\right)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1})^{\frac{r}{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\rho_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H_{x}^{1}}^{2}\right)^{r}\right],

for
r>1
, where
C
depends on
\omega
and
r
.
By Kolmogorov-Centov’s continuity theorem (see §
4.
in Appendix), up to a modification, there holds

(2.89)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\rho_{m}\Pi_{m}(\mathbb{F}(\rho_{m},\mathbf{u}_{m}))\operatorname{d}W_{m}\right\|_{C^{0,\varsigma}([0,T];L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right))}\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

uniformly in
m
, for
r>2,\varsigma\in\left(0,\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{r}\right)
. Combined with the result of deterministic part (
2.3
), with
C
dependent on
\omega
,
T
and
r
, it holds

(2.90)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\Pi_{m}\left(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}\right)\right\|_{C^{0,\varsigma}\left([0,T];L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)}\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\varsigma\in\left(0,\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{r}\right),\quad r>4.

The embedding
C^{0,\varsigma}\left([0,T];W^{-l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\hookrightarrow C^{0,\varsigma-\varepsilon}\left([0,T];W^{-k,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
,
k>l,

implies the tightness of
\mathcal{L}\left[\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}\right]
in
C^{0,\varsigma}\left([0,T];W^{-k,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
, for
k>l>\frac{5}{2}
.

Since the quantum term provides a better regularity in the energy estimates, we obtain

\Pi_{m}\left(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}\right)\in L^{\infty}\left([0,T];L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\cap C^{0,\varsigma}\left([0,T];W^{-k,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
,
which is compactly embedded in
C\left([0,T];L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
.
In fact,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\Pi_{m}\left(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{2})-\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{1})\right)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\right)^{r}\right]

(2.91)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sup\limits_{\phi\in L^{3}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right),\left\|\phi\right\|_{L^{3}}=1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\Pi_{m}\left(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{2})-\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{1})\right)\phi\operatorname{d}x\right)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sup\limits_{\phi\in L^{3}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right),\left\|\phi\right\|_{L^{3}}=1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\Pi_{m}\left(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{2})-\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{1})\right)\left(\phi-\phi\ast\eta_{\imath}\right)\operatorname{d}x\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\left.\quad+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\Pi_{m}\left(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{2})-\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{1})\right)\phi\ast\eta_{\imath}\operatorname{d}x\right)\right)^{r}\right],

where
\eta_{\imath}
is a mollifier and
\left\|\phi\ast\eta_{\imath}\right\|_{L^{3}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\rightarrow\left\|\phi\right\|_{L^{3}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}
holds as
\imath
goes to
0
. Since
L^{p}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right),1<p<\infty
is uniformly convex space, by Hanner’s inequality, weak convergence and convergence in norm, we imply that
\left\|\phi\ast\eta_{\imath}-\phi\right\|_{L^{3}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\rightarrow 0
. By Hölder’s inequality, the first term in the above formula will go to
0
. By stochastic Fubini’s theorem, it holds

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\Pi_{m}\left(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{2})-\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{1})\right)\phi\ast\eta_{\imath}\operatorname{d}x

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\operatorname{d}Y_{m}(t)+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\rho_{m}\mathds{F}(\rho_{m},\mathbf{u}_{m})\operatorname{d}W_{m}\right)\phi\ast\eta_{\imath}\operatorname{d}x

(2.92)

\displaystyle=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\operatorname{d}Y_{m}(t)}{\operatorname{d}t}\phi\ast\eta_{\imath}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{m}\mathds{F}(\rho_{m},\mathbf{u}_{m})\phi\ast\eta_{\imath}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}W_{m}

\displaystyle\leqslant\left\|\frac{\operatorname{d}Y_{m}(t)}{\operatorname{d}t}\right\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}([0,T];W^{-l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right))}\left\|\phi\ast\eta_{\imath}\right\|_{W^{l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}(t_{2}-t_{1})^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\rho_{m}\mathds{F}(\rho_{m},\mathbf{u}_{m})\operatorname{d}W_{m}\left(\phi\ast\eta_{\imath}\right)\operatorname{d}x.

With
C
dependent on
T
and
r
, it holds

(2.93)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\rho_{m}\mathbb{F}(\rho_{m},\mathbf{u}_{m})\operatorname{d}W_{m}\right\|^{r}_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\rho_{m}\mathbb{F}(\rho_{m},\mathbf{u}_{m})\operatorname{d}W_{m}\right\|^{r}_{L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\right]\leqslant C(t_{2}-t_{1})^{\frac{r}{2}}.

Therefore, we have

(2.94)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\Pi_{m}\left(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{2})-\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{1})\right)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C(t_{2}-t_{1})^{\frac{r}{4}},

by Kolmogorov-Centov’s theorem, up to a modification, it holds

(2.95)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\Pi_{m}\left(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t)\right)\right\|_{C^{0,\varsigma}([0,T];L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

uniformly in
m
, where
C
depends on
T
and
r
, for
\varsigma<\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{r}
. Alternatively, by Chebyshev’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{m\geqslant 1}\mathbb{P}_{m}\left[\left\{\left\|\Pi_{m}\left(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{2})-\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{1})\right)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}>\varepsilon\right\}\right]

(2.96)

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{m\geqslant 1}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\Pi_{m}\left(\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{2})-\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m}(t_{1})\right)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\right)^{r}\right]}{\varepsilon^{r}}

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C\delta^{\frac{r}{4}}\rightarrow 0,\quad\left|t_{2}-t_{1}\right|\leqslant\delta,\text{ as }\delta\rightarrow 0,

by Kolmogorov’s theorem (see §
5.
in Appendix), we get the tightness.
This completes the proof.
\square

Step 3:
Pass to the limit
m\rightarrow\infty
.

\mathcal{X}_{2}
is sub-Polish space instead of Polish space because weak topologies are generally not measurable. So our stochastic compactness argument
is based on Jakubowski’s extension of Skorokhod’s representation theorem.

Proposition 2.8
.

There exist

\left\{{\bar{\rho}_{0,m}},\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,m},\frac{\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,m}}{\sqrt{\bar{\rho}_{0,m}}},\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m},\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m},\bar{W}_{m}\right\},\quad m\in\mathds{N},

and

\left\{\rho_{0,{\rm reg}},\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm reg}},\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm reg}}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0,{\rm reg}}}},\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg},\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg},W_{\rm reg}\right\}
, two families of
\mathcal{X}_{2}
-valued Borel measurable random variables defined on a complete probability space, still denoted as
\left(\bar{\Omega},\bar{\mathcal{F}},\bar{\mathbb{P}}\right)
, such that (up to a subsequence):

(1)

For all
m\in\mathds{N}
,

\mathcal{L}\left[\bar{\rho}_{0,m},\bar{q}_{0,m},\frac{\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,m}}{\sqrt{\bar{\rho}_{0,m}}},\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m},\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m},\bar{W}_{m}\right],\quad m\in\mathds{N}

coincides with
\mathcal{L}\left[\rho_{0,m},\mathbf{q}_{0,m},\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,m}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0,m}}},\rho_{m},\mathbf{u}_{m},\rho_{m}\mathbf{u}_{m},W_{m}\right],\quad m\in\mathds{N}
on
\mathcal{X}_{2}
;

(2)

\mathcal{L}\left[\rho_{0,{\rm reg}},\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm reg}},\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm reg}}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0,{\rm reg}}}},\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg},\rho_{\rm{reg}}\mathbf{u}_{\rm{reg}},W_{\rm{reg}}\right]
on
\mathcal{X}_{2}
is a Radon measure;

(3)

\left\{\bar{\rho}_{0,m},\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,m},\frac{\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,m}}{\sqrt{\bar{\rho}_{0,m}}},\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m},\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m},\bar{W}_{m}\right\}
,
m\in\mathds{N}
,
converges to

\left\{\rho_{0,{\rm reg}},\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm reg}},\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm reg}}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0,{\rm reg}}}},\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg},\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg},W_{\rm reg}\right\}
in the topology of
\mathcal{X}_{2}
,
\bar{\mathbb{P}}

a.s.
as
m\rightarrow+\infty
.

Corollary 2.1
.

L^{2}\left(0,T;W^{2,\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
is continuously embedded in
L^{2}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
and

L^{2}\left(0,T;L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
.
Since
W^{2,\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
and
L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
are reflexive spaces, applying the Aubin-Lion’s lemma,
we have the strong convergence of
\rho_{m}
:

(2.97)

\bar{\rho}_{m}\rightarrow\rho_{\rm reg}\quad\text{in}\quad L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{2}\left([0,T];W^{1,3}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right),\quad r\geqslant 2.

In addition, by (
2.68
),
\bar{\rho}_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\in L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{2}\left([0,T];H^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
,
H^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\hookrightarrow W^{1,6}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,
W^{1,6}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
is continuously embedded
in
L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
, therefore, the strong convergence of
\bar{\rho}_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}
holds:

(2.98)

\bar{\rho}_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\rightarrow\rho_{\rm reg}^{\frac{1}{2}}\quad\text{in}\quad L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{2}\left([0,T];W^{1,6}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right),\quad r\geqslant 2.

Remark 2.1
.

\left\|\Pi_{m}\left[\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\right]\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}
is continuous in
[0,T]
uniformly in
m
,
\bar{\mathbb{P}}

a.s.

The compactness of
\{\Pi_{m}\left[\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\right]\}
yields that there exists
\mathbf{q}_{\rm{reg}}
in
C\left([0,T];L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
such that

(2.99)

\left\|\Pi_{m}\left[\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\right]\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\rightarrow\left\|\mathbf{q}_{\rm reg}\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\text{ in }C([0,T]),\quad\bar{\mathbb{P}}\text{ {\rm a.s.}}

Since
L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
is an uniformly convex space, therefore we have

(2.100)

\Pi_{m}\left[\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\right]\rightarrow\mathbf{q}_{\rm reg}\text{ in }C\left([0,T];L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right),\quad\bar{\mathbb{P}}\text{ {\rm a.s.}}

Hence
\mathcal{L}\left[\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\right]
is tight in
\mathcal{X}_{\rho\mathbf{u}}
. Since
\left\{\bar{\rho}_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\right\}
converges weakly in
L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{\infty}\left([0,T];L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
, (
2.65
),

\bar{\rho}_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\rightarrow\rho_{\rm reg}^{\frac{1}{2}}
in
L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{2}\left([0,T];W^{1,6}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
, in
L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{\infty}\left([0,T];L^{6}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)

\left\{\bar{\rho}_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}
converges weakly to
\rho_{\rm reg}^{\frac{1}{2}}
,

\mathbb{E}\left[r_{0}^{r}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\right\|^{4r}_{L_{t}^{4}L_{x}^{4}}\right]
\leqslant C
,
C
depends on
T
,
r
and
r_{0}
,
so
\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\rightharpoonup\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}
in
L^{r}\left(\Omega;C\left([0,T];L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
. Therefore
\mathbf{q}_{\rm reg}=\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}
in
C\left([0,T];L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
,
\bar{\mathbb{P}}

a.s.

It is worth mentioning is that whether the stochastic integral
\int_{0}^{T}\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbb{F}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\operatorname{d}W_{\rm reg}
makes sense or not. Firstly, we know

\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{T}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbb{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\operatorname{d}x\right)^{2}\operatorname{d}t\right]<+\infty
.
\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right)
is adapted in the canonical filtration
\tilde{\mathcal{F}}=\sigma\left(\sigma[\rho_{\rm reg}(t)]\bigcup\sigma[\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}(t)]\bigcup\sigma[W_{\rm reg}(t)]\right)
; by the property of
\rho_{\rm reg}\in C\left([0,T];L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
and
\left|\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\right|\leqslant f_{k}\left(\left|\rho_{\rm reg}\right|+\left|\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|\right)
, we have that the integral
\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\operatorname{d}x
is continuous in time, therefore it is progressively measurable, see §
6.
in Appendix. Hence the the stochastic integral
\int_{0}^{T}\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbb{F}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\operatorname{d}W_{\rm reg}
is well-defined.

In this
m\rightarrow\infty
layer, the damping terms provide
\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\rightharpoonup\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}
weakly in
L^{4}([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3})
,

\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\rightarrow\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}
strongly in
C\left([0,T];L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
,

\bar{\rho}_{m}\rightharpoonup\rho_{\rm reg}
weakly in
L^{2}\left([0,T];H^{9}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
,
so
\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}
satisfy the continuity equation in a weak sense by Vitali’s convergence theorem. With the weak convergence of

p(\bar{\rho}_{m}),\bar{\rho}_{0,m}^{-10}
,
\nabla\triangle^{4}\bar{\rho}_{0,m}
,

\nabla\bar{\rho}_{m}^{-5}
,
\triangle^{5}\bar{\rho}_{m}
,

\nabla\bar{\rho}_{m}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}
,
\bar{\rho}_{m}\nabla^{2}\ln\bar{\rho}_{m}
in the corresponding spaces, we have the convergence of deterministic terms in approximated momentum equation.
We mainly consider the convergence of
\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{T}\Pi_{m}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m}\Pi_{m}\left(\mathbb{F}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\right)\right)\operatorname{d}\bar{W}_{m}\operatorname{d}x.

Lemma 2.4
.

\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{T}\Pi_{m}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m}\Pi_{m}\left(\mathbb{F}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\right)\right)\operatorname{d}\bar{W}_{m}\operatorname{d}x\rightarrow\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{T}\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbb{F}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\operatorname{d}W_{\rm reg}\operatorname{d}x

\bar{\mathbb{P}}

a.s.

Proof.

We first claim that
\rho_{\rm reg}>0
for almost everywhere
\left(\omega,t,x\right)\in\Omega\times(0,T)\times\mathbb{T}^{3}
.

Since
\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}^{-1}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{\infty}}\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|D^{2}\rho_{\rm reg}^{-1}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{2}}\right)^{r}\right]
, computing

(2.101)

D^{2}\rho_{\rm reg}^{-1}=D\left(D\left(\rho_{\rm reg}^{-1}\right)\right)=D\left(-\rho_{\rm reg}^{-2}D\rho_{\rm reg}\right)=2\rho_{\rm reg}^{-3}D\rho_{\rm reg}\otimes D\rho_{\rm reg}-\rho_{\rm reg}^{-2}D^{2}\rho_{\rm reg},\\

so by Hölder’s inequalities, we have

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}^{-1}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{\infty}}^{r}\right]\leqslant C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}^{-3}|D\rho_{\rm reg}|^{2}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{2}}^{r}+\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}^{-2}|D^{2}\rho_{\rm reg}|\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{2}}^{r}\right]

(2.102)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}^{-1}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{10}}^{3r}\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H_{x}^{9}}^{2r}\right]+C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}^{-1}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{10}}^{2r}\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H_{x}^{9}}^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(t)^{\frac{3r}{10}}e_{\rm reg}(t)^{r}\right]+C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(t)^{\frac{2r}{10}}e_{\rm reg}(t)^{\frac{r}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{r}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{\frac{13r}{10}}\right]+\left(\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{\frac{13r}{10}}\right]\right)^{\frac{7}{13}}\right),

for some
r>4
, where
C_{r}
may be different constants in different inequality. The last inequality holds up to a constant
K^{r}
, which comes from Burkholder-Davis-Gundy’s inequality, which is equivalent to
e^{r}
, see §
3.
in Appendix. By Chebyshev’s inequality, it holds

(2.103)

\displaystyle\bar{\mathbb{P}}\left[\left\{\omega\in\Omega\left|\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}^{-1}(\omega)\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{\infty}}>L\right.\right\}\right]\leqslant\frac{C_{r}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{\frac{13r}{10}}\right]+\left(\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{\frac{13r}{10}}\right]\right)^{\frac{7}{13}}\right)}{L^{r}}\rightarrow 0,

as
L\rightarrow+\infty
,
which shows that
\rho_{\rm reg}>0
for almost everywhere in
\Omega\times(0,T)\times\mathbb{T}^{3}

\mathbb{P}
a.s.

Now we turn to the proof of lemma
2.4
.
Recalling the definition of
\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})
, when
\rho_{\rm reg}\neq 0
,
we write
\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})=\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\rho_{\rm reg},\frac{\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}}{\rho_{\rm reg}}\right)
.
Recall that
|\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}|\leqslant f_{k}(\left|\rho_{\rm reg}\right|+\left|\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|)
,
\sum\limits_{k=1}^{\infty}f_{k}^{2}<+\infty
.
Defining
O^{\iota}=\left\{(t,x)|\rho_{\rm reg}<\iota\right\}
,
for
\rho_{\rm reg}
in
(O^{\iota})^{c}
, when
m
large enough, it holds
\rho_{m}>\frac{\iota}{2}
.
From Remark
2.1
, we know that
\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}
converges strongly to
\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}
in
C\left([0,T];L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
,
\bar{\rho}_{m}\rightarrow\rho_{\rm reg}
strongly in
C\left([0,T];W^{1,3}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
, therefore they converge almost everywhere up to a subsequence.
We claim that
\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}}{\bar{\rho}_{m}}\right)\rightarrow\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\rho_{\rm reg},\frac{\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}}{\rho_{\rm reg}}\right)
due to the strong convergence of
\bar{\rho}_{m}\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}}{\bar{\rho}_{m}})\rightarrow\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\frac{\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}}{\rho_{\rm reg}})
.
Actually, for the situation in
O^{\iota}
,
\rho_{\rm reg}\neq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\bar{\rho}_{m}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}}{\bar{\rho}_{m}}\right)-\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\rho_{\rm reg},\frac{\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}}{\rho_{\rm reg}}\right)

(2.104)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bar{\rho}_{m}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}}{\bar{\rho}_{m}}\right)-\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}}{\bar{\rho}_{m}}\right)

\displaystyle+\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}}{\bar{\rho}_{m}}\right)-\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\rho_{\rm reg},\frac{\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}}{\rho_{\rm reg}}\right),

so we get

\displaystyle\left|\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}}{\bar{\rho}_{m}}\right)-\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\rho_{\rm reg},\frac{\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}}{\rho_{\rm reg}}\right)\right|

(2.105)

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle\left|\bar{\rho}_{m}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}}{\bar{\rho}_{m}}\right)-\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}}{\bar{\rho}_{m}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\bar{\rho}_{m}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}}{\bar{\rho}_{m}}\right)-\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}}{\bar{\rho}_{m}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle f_{k}\left(\left|\bar{\rho}_{m}-\rho_{\rm reg}\right|+\left|\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}-\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|\right)+\left|\bar{\rho}_{m}-\rho_{\rm reg}\right|\left|\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}}{\bar{\rho}_{m}}\right)\right|.

Therefore, for
\rho_{\rm reg}>0
, we have

(2.106)

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}}{\bar{\rho}_{m}}\right)-\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\rho_{\rm reg},\frac{\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}}{{\rho}}\right)\right|\rightarrow 0,\text{ almost everywhere as }m\rightarrow+\infty.

When
\rho_{\rm reg}=0
,
\left|\bar{\rho}_{m}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}}{\bar{\rho}_{m}}\right)\right|\leqslant f_{k}\left(|\bar{\rho}_{m}|+\left|\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\right|\right)
,
this implies
\left|\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{m}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}}{\bar{\rho}_{m}}\right)-0\right|\leqslant f_{k}\left(1+\left|\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\right|\right)
, hence the integral is bounded, which gives that

(2.107)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\rho_{\rm reg}=0}\left|\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\right)-\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t=0\text{ as }m\rightarrow+\infty.

So (
2.107
) along with (
2.106
) yields

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{O^{\iota}}|\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})-\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t

(2.108)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{O^{\iota}\cap\{\rho_{\rm reg}=0\}}|\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})-\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{O^{\iota}\cap\{\rho_{\rm reg}\neq 0\}}|\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})-\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t\rightarrow 0\text{ as }m\rightarrow+\infty.

Therefore, it holds

(2.109)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})-\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{O^{\iota}}|\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})-\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t+\int_{(O^{\iota})^{c}}|\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})-\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle\rightarrow 0\text{ as }m\rightarrow+\infty.

Consequently, we have

(2.110)

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\rightarrow\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\quad\text{in}\quad L^{2}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right).

Therefore,
\Pi_{m}\left(\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\right)
converges to
\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})
in
L^{2}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
due to the continuity of projection operator
\Pi_{m}
.
On the other hand, we have the strong convergence of
\bar{\rho}_{m}
in
L^{2}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
, so

(2.111)

\Pi_{m}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m}\Pi_{m}\left(\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\right)\right)\rightarrow\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\text{ in }L^{1}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right).

The strong convergence of

\Pi_{m}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m}\Pi_{m}\left(\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\right)\right)\right)
in
L^{2}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
is required in the convergence of the stochastic integral. Since

\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\Pi_{m}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m}\mathbb{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\right)\right)^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t
is bounded,
so we get
\bar{\rho}_{m}\Pi_{m}\left(\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\right)\right)\rightarrow\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right)
in
L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{2}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
.
By dominated convergence theorem, the strong convergence of
\bar{\rho}_{m}
, and
\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{\infty}}\leqslant\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H_{x}^{9}}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\bar{\rho}_{m}\Pi_{m}\left(\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\right)-\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\bar{\rho}_{m}\Pi_{m}\left[\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\right]-\rho_{\rm reg}\Pi_{m}\left(\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\right)\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t

(2.112)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+2\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\rho_{\rm reg}\Pi_{m}\left(\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\right)-\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle 2f_{k}^{2}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\bar{\rho}_{m}-\rho_{\rm reg}|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle+\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{\infty}}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\Pi_{m}\left(\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\right)-\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle\rightarrow 0\text{ as }m\rightarrow+\infty.

This shows that

(2.113)

\Pi_{m}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m}\Pi_{m}\left(\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\right)\right)\rightarrow\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\text{ in }L^{2}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right).

Now combined with (
2.113
) and
\bar{W}_{m}\rightarrow W_{\rm reg}
, by lemma
2.1
, it holds

\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{T}\Pi_{m}\left(\bar{\rho}_{m}\Pi_{m}\left(\mathbf{F}_{k}(\bar{\rho}_{m},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\right)\right)\operatorname{d}\bar{W}_{m}\operatorname{d}x\rightarrow\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{T}\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\operatorname{d}W_{\rm reg}\operatorname{d}x
as
m\rightarrow+\infty

\bar{\mathbb{P}}
a.s. This completes the proof of the lemma.
\square

Notice that even
\eta
and
\delta
will vanish finally, by the Lipschitz continuity of
\rho\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho,\mathbf{u})
, the convergence of
\rho\mathbf{F}_{k}(\rho,\mathbf{u})
still holds without good regularity of
\rho
.

Step 4:

\left(\left(\bar{\Omega},\bar{\mathcal{F}},\bar{\mathbb{P}}\right),\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg},W_{\rm reg}\right)
is a martingale solution after taking the limit.

By Vitali’s convergence theorem and lemma
2.4
, we have the following proposition:

Proposition 2.9
.

\left(\left(\bar{\Omega},\bar{\mathcal{F}},\bar{\mathbb{P}}\right),\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg},W_{\rm reg}\right)
is a martingale solution to the system

(2.114)

\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\vskip 5.16663pt\left(\rho_{\rm reg}\right)_{t}+\operatorname{div}(\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})=\varepsilon\triangle\rho_{\rm reg},\\
\operatorname{d}\left(\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right)+\left(\operatorname{div}(\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\otimes\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})+\nabla\left(a\rho_{\rm reg}^{\gamma}\right)-\operatorname{div}(\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbb{D}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\right)\operatorname{d}t\\
=\left(-r_{0}|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}|^{2}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}-r_{1}\rho|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}|^{2}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}-r_{2}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right)\operatorname{d}t\\
\quad+\left(\frac{11}{10}\eta\nabla\rho_{\rm reg}^{-10}+\delta\rho_{\rm reg}\nabla\triangle^{9}\rho_{\rm reg}+\kappa\rho_{\rm reg}\left(\nabla\left(\frac{\triangle\sqrt{\rho_{\rm reg}}}{\sqrt{\rho_{\rm reg}}}\right)\right)\right)\operatorname{d}t\\
\quad-\left(\varepsilon\nabla\rho_{\rm reg}\nabla\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right)\operatorname{d}t-\left(\varepsilon\triangle^{2}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right)\operatorname{d}t+\left(\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbb{F}(\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg})\right)\operatorname{d}W_{\rm reg}.\end{array}\right.

We denote the energy as

(2.115)

\displaystyle e_{\rm reg}(t)=\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\rho_{\rm reg}|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}|^{2}+\frac{a}{\gamma}\rho_{\rm reg}^{\gamma}+\frac{\eta}{10}\rho_{\rm reg}^{-10}+\frac{\kappa}{2}|\nabla\sqrt{\rho_{\rm reg}}|^{2}+\frac{\delta}{2}|\nabla\triangle^{4}\rho_{\rm reg}|^{2}\right)\operatorname{d}x,

then we compute the estimates

(2.116)

\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(t)^{r}\right]\leqslant\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right],r>4,

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\varepsilon\frac{4a}{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\nabla\rho_{\rm reg}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s+\varepsilon\eta\frac{11}{25}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\nabla\rho_{\rm reg}^{-5}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s+\varepsilon\delta\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\triangle^{5}\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.\left.+\varepsilon\kappa\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\nabla^{2}\ln\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\int_{0}^{t}+\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\triangle\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\mathbb{D}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right.\right.

(2.117)

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(r_{0}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|^{4}+r_{1}\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|^{4}+r_{2}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\right)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant C\left(1+\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right]\right),

for
C
depends on
r
and
T
.

3.
Vanishing artificial viscosity limit:
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0

Before sending
\varepsilon
to
0
, we need to develop the stochastic B-D entropy with uniform energy estimates independent of
\kappa,\varepsilon
. In order to construct the energy estimates uniformly in
\varepsilon
, we first give the stochastic B-D entropy.

3.1.
The stochastic B-D entropy estimates

In this subsection we define the approximated B-D entropy

(3.1)

\displaystyle\tilde{e}_{\rm reg}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}+\nabla\ln\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}+\int_{1}^{\rho_{\rm reg}}\frac{p(z)}{z}\operatorname{d}z\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad+\frac{\eta}{10}\rho_{\rm reg}^{-10}+\frac{\delta}{2}\left|\nabla\triangle^{4}\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}+\frac{\kappa}{2}\left|\nabla\sqrt{\rho_{\rm reg}}\right|^{2}+r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\ln_{-}\rho_{\rm reg}\right)\operatorname{d}x,

and we have the following lemma.

Lemma 3.1
.

We conclude the following estimates: there hold

(3.2)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\tilde{e}_{\rm reg}(t)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant C,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\varepsilon\frac{4a}{\gamma}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\nabla\rho_{\rm reg}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\varepsilon\eta\frac{11}{25}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\nabla\rho_{\rm reg}^{-5}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\varepsilon\delta\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\triangle^{5}\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\nabla\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}-\nabla\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}^{\top}\right|^{2}\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\varepsilon\kappa\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\nabla^{2}\ln\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(r_{0}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|^{4}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

(3.3)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(r_{1}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|^{4}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(r_{2}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\eta\frac{11}{25}\left|\nabla\rho_{\rm reg}^{-5}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\gamma\rho_{\rm reg}^{\gamma}\left|\nabla\ln\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\delta\left|\triangle^{5}\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\kappa\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\nabla^{2}\ln\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

where
C
depends on
\delta,\eta,r,T,r_{0},r_{1},\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right],\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{\frac{6r}{5}}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{k_{{\rm I}}r}\right]
,
\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right]
,
i=1,2,3
,
r>4
.

Proof.
For convenience, we denote
\rho_{\rm reg}
as
\rho
and denote
\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}
as
\mathbf{u}
in this process of proof.
Denoting
f\triangleq f(\zeta,q)=\rho\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho=q\zeta
,
q=\rho\mathbf{u}
,
f_{\zeta}=q=\rho\mathbf{u},f_{q}=\zeta
,
f_{qq}=0
, by Itô’s formula, it holds

(3.4)

\operatorname{d}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right)=\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\operatorname{d}\left(\rho\mathbf{u}\right)\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\mathbf{u}\cdot\operatorname{d}\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x.\\

The first term reduces to

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\operatorname{d}(\rho\mathbf{u})\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}-\operatorname{div}\left(\rho\mathbf{u}\otimes\mathbf{u}\right)\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla\rho^{\gamma}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\operatorname{div}\left(\rho\mathbb{D}\mathbf{u}\right)\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{11}{10}\eta\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla\rho^{-10}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x+\delta\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\nabla\triangle^{9}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x+\kappa\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\operatorname{div}\left(\rho\nabla^{2}\ln\rho\right)\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right.

(3.5)

\displaystyle\left.+\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\nabla\rho\nabla\mathbf{u}\right)\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x-\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\triangle^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right.

\displaystyle\left.-r_{0}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\mathbf{u}|^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x-r_{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho|\mathbf{u}|^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x-r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\mathbb{F}(\rho,\rho\mathbf{u})\operatorname{d}W\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x.

The second term in (
3.4
) is written as

(3.6)

\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\mathbf{u}\cdot\operatorname{d}(\nabla\ln\rho)\operatorname{d}x=\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\operatorname{div}\left(\rho\mathbf{u}\right)\frac{\operatorname{div}\left(\rho\mathbf{u}\right)-\varepsilon\triangle\rho}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t.

From the mass equation, it holds

(3.7)

\displaystyle\quad\operatorname{d}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\rho\left|\nabla\ln\rho\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\right)

\displaystyle=\left(-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\nabla\mathbf{u}:\nabla\ln\rho\otimes\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\triangle\rho\operatorname{div}\mathbf{u}\operatorname{d}x+\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla\triangle\rho}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}t.

After the simplification of the deterministic part
[
39
,
7
]
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\operatorname{d}\left(\rho\mathbf{u}\right)\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\mathbf{u}\cdot\operatorname{d}\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x+\operatorname{d}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\rho\left|\nabla\ln\rho\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\left|\mathbb{D}\mathbf{u}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\rho}{2}\left|\nabla\mathbf{u}-\nabla\mathbf{u}^{\top}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla\rho^{\gamma}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right.

(3.8)

\displaystyle\quad\left.+\frac{11}{10}\eta\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla\rho^{-10}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x+\delta\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\nabla\triangle^{9}\rho\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.+\kappa\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\operatorname{div}\left(\rho\nabla^{2}\ln\rho\right)\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x+\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\nabla\rho\nabla\mathbf{u}\right)\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.+\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla\triangle\rho}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x-\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\operatorname{div}\left(\rho\mathbf{u}\right)\triangle\rho}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x-\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\triangle^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.-r_{0}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\mathbf{u}\right|^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x-r_{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\left|\mathbf{u}\right|^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x-r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\mathbb{F}(\rho,\rho\mathbf{u})\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}W.

Together with the energy balance of
\operatorname{d}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\rho\mathbf{u}^{2}\operatorname{d}x\right)
(
2.2.2
), it follows

\displaystyle\operatorname{d}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left[\frac{1}{2}\rho\left|\mathbf{u}+\nabla\ln\rho\right|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{10}\rho^{-10}+\frac{\delta}{2}\left|\nabla\triangle^{4}\rho\right|^{2}+\frac{\kappa}{2}\left|\nabla\sqrt{\rho}\right|^{2}+\int_{1}^{\rho}\frac{p(z)}{z}\operatorname{d}z\right]\operatorname{d}x\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\varepsilon\frac{4a}{\gamma}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\nabla\rho^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x+\frac{11}{25}\varepsilon\eta\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\nabla\rho^{-5}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x+\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\triangle\mathbf{u}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x+\varepsilon\kappa\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\left|\nabla^{2}\ln\rho\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\right.

(3.9)

\displaystyle\left.\quad+r_{0}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\mathbf{u}\right|^{4}\operatorname{d}x+r_{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\mathbf{u}^{4}\operatorname{d}x+r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\mathbf{u}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\rho}{2}\left|\nabla\mathbf{u}-\nabla\mathbf{u}^{\top}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle+\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\gamma\rho^{\gamma}\left|\nabla\ln\rho\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{11}{25}\eta\left|\nabla\rho^{-5}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x+\kappa\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\left|\nabla^{2}\ln\rho\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t+\delta\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\triangle^{5}\rho\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle=\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\mathbb{F}\left(\rho,\mathbf{u}\right)\cdot\mathbf{u}\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}W+\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\rho\left|\mathbb{F}\left(\rho,\mathbf{u}\right)\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}t+\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\mathbb{F}(\rho,\rho\mathbf{u})\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}W

\displaystyle+\left(\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\nabla\rho\nabla\triangle\rho}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x+\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla\rho\nabla\mathbf{u}\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t-\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\operatorname{div}(\rho\mathbf{u})\frac{\triangle\rho}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x-\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\triangle\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\triangle\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad-r_{0}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\mathbf{u}\right|^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x-r_{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\left|\mathbf{u}\right|^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x-r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}+I_{4}+I_{5}+I_{6}+I_{7}+I_{8}+I_{9}+I_{10},

where
\displaystyle\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\gamma\rho^{\gamma}\left|\nabla\ln\rho\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}t=\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\nabla\rho^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}t=\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla\rho^{\gamma}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}t
.

For
I_{1}=\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\mathbb{F}\left(\rho,\mathbf{u}\right)\cdot\mathbf{u}\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}W
, similarly as (
2.2.2
), we have

(3.10)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\mathbb{F}\left(\rho,\mathbf{u}\right)\cdot\mathbf{u}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}W\right|^{r}\right]\leqslant C\left(\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right]+1\right),\quad r>4,

with
