depends on
r,t
and
\sum\limits_{k=1}^{+\infty}f^{2}_{k}
.

For term
\displaystyle I_{2}=\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\rho|\mathbb{F}\left(\rho,\mathbf{u}\right)|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}s
, similarly as (
2.2.2
), we estimate

(3.11)

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\rho|\mathbb{F}\left(\rho,\mathbf{u}\right)|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right|^{r}\right]\leqslant C\left(\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right]+1\right),

where
C
depends on
r,t
and
\sum\limits_{k=1}^{+\infty}f^{2}_{k}
.

For
\displaystyle I_{3}=\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\mathbb{F}(\rho,\mathbf{u})\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}W
, it holds

(3.12)

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\mathbb{F}(\rho,\mathbf{u})\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}W\right|^{r}\right]\leqslant C\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\left|\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\mathbb{F}(\rho,\mathbf{u})\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}s\right)^{\frac{r}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant C\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\sum\limits_{k=1}^{+\infty}f_{k}^{2}\left(\left\|\rho\nabla\ln\rho\right\|_{L^{1}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}^{2}+\left\|\left|\rho\mathbf{u}\right|\left|\nabla\ln\rho\right|\right\|_{L^{1}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}^{2}\right)\operatorname{d}s\right)^{\frac{r}{2}}\right],

where
C
depends on
r
and
\sum\limits_{k=1}^{+\infty}f^{2}_{k}
. Based on (
2.116
), by Hölder’s inequality and Cauchy’s inequality, it holds

(3.13)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left|\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\sqrt{\rho}\sqrt{\rho}\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}s\leqslant\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\operatorname{d}x\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\left|\nabla\ln\rho\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C\sup\limits_{s\in[0,t]}(e_{\rm reg}(s)+1)\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho|\nabla\ln\rho|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s,

so we have

(3.14)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\left|\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\sqrt{\rho}\sqrt{\rho}\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}s\right)^{\frac{r}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C(\mathbb{E}[e_{\rm reg}(0)]^{r}+1)+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho|\nabla\ln\rho|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right],

where
C
depends on
r,t
and
\sum\limits_{k=1}^{+\infty}f^{2}_{k}
,
r>4
.

(3.15)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\rho\mathbf{u}\right|\left|\nabla\ln\rho\right|\operatorname{d}x\right)^{2}\operatorname{d}s\leqslant\int_{0}^{t}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\left|\mathbf{u}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\right)\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\left|\nabla\ln\rho\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C\sup\limits_{s\in[0,t]}(e_{\rm reg}(s)+1)\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho|\nabla\ln\rho|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s,

Hence we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}I_{3}\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant

\displaystyle C(\mathbb{E}[e_{\rm reg}(0)]^{r}+1)+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho|\nabla\ln\rho|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right].

For the term
\displaystyle I_{4}=\varepsilon\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla\triangle\rho}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}s
, for
\kappa>0
, since
\sqrt{\varepsilon\delta}\triangle^{5}\rho\in L^{2}([0,t]\times\mathbb{T}^{3})
,

H^{10}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\hookrightarrow C^{8,\gamma}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
, we do the following estimate

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{0}^{t}\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\nabla\rho\nabla\triangle\rho}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right|^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\rho^{-1}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{10}}\int_{0}^{t}\varepsilon\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla\triangle\rho\right|^{\frac{10}{9}}\operatorname{d}x\right)^{\frac{9}{10}}\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\rho^{-1}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{10}}\left\|\nabla\sqrt{\rho}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{2}}\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\nabla\triangle\rho\right|^{\frac{5}{2}}\operatorname{d}x\right)^{\frac{2}{5}}\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]

(3.16)

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\rho^{-1}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{10}}\left\|\nabla\sqrt{\rho}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{2}}\right)^{r}\left(\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\nabla\triangle\rho\right|^{5}\operatorname{d}x\right)^{\frac{1}{5}}\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\rho^{-1}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{10}}\left\|\nabla\sqrt{\rho}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{2}}\right)^{r}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\delta^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|(\varepsilon\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}}\triangle^{5}\rho\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{r}{2}}\delta^{-\frac{r}{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\rho^{-1}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{10}}\left\|\nabla\sqrt{\rho}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{2}}\right)^{r}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|(\varepsilon\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}}\triangle^{5}\rho\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}s\right)^{\frac{r}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{r}{2}}\delta^{-\frac{r}{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\sup\limits_{s\in[0,t]}e_{\rm reg}(s)^{\frac{6r}{5}}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|(\varepsilon\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}}\triangle^{5}\rho\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C\varepsilon^{\frac{r}{2}},

where
C
depends on
\delta,\eta,\kappa,r,t
and
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{\frac{6r}{5}}\right]
, with the upper bound

\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\sup\limits_{s\in[0,t]}e_{\rm reg}(s)^{\frac{6r}{5}}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leqslant C\left(\left(\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{\frac{6r}{5}}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+1\right).

Notice that the quantum term is indispensable for the regularized system because it is necessary for the estimate of
I_{4}
, which arises from the artificial viscosity
\varepsilon\triangle\rho
.

For
\displaystyle I_{5}=\varepsilon\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla\rho\nabla\mathbf{u}\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}s
, we treat it similarly to
[
41
]
, the process is still given here. By Sobolev-Poincaré’s inequalities on torus,
\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla\mathbf{u}\operatorname{d}x=0
, we have
\left\|\nabla\mathbf{u}\right\|_{L^{6}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\leqslant C\left\|\triangle\mathbf{u}\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}
,

(3.17)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\varepsilon\nabla\rho\nabla\mathbf{u}\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right|\leqslant\varepsilon C\left\|\nabla\mathbf{u}\right\|_{L^{6}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\left\|\rho^{-1}\right\|_{L^{10}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\left\|\rho\right\|^{2}_{W^{1,\frac{30}{11}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}

\displaystyle\leqslant\varepsilon C\left\|\triangle\mathbf{u}\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\left\|\rho^{-1}\right\|_{L^{10}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\left\|\rho\right\|^{2}_{W^{1,\frac{30}{11}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)},

by
C_{r}
-inequality.
Hence by Hölder’s inequality and Cauchy’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\varepsilon\nabla\rho\nabla\mathbf{u}\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]

(3.18)

\displaystyle\leqslant\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\varepsilon C\left\|\triangle\mathbf{u}\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\left\|\rho^{-1}\right\|_{L^{10}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\left\|\rho\right\|^{2}_{W^{1,\frac{30}{11}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\varepsilon\left(\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\triangle\mathbf{u}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\operatorname{d}s\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\rho^{-1}\right\|^{2}_{L^{10}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\left\|\rho\right\|^{3}_{H^{9}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\left\|\rho\right\|_{H^{10}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\operatorname{d}s\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{\varepsilon^{r}}{3}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\triangle\mathbf{u}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]+\varepsilon^{r}3\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\rho^{-1}\right\|^{2}_{L^{10}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\left\|\rho\right\|^{3}_{H^{9}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\left\|\rho\right\|_{H^{10}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{\varepsilon^{r}}{3}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\triangle\mathbf{u}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]+C\varepsilon^{\frac{r}{2}},

where
C
depends on
\delta,\eta,r,t
and
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{k_{1}r}\right]
, in which
k_{1}
is a specific positive constant. The last inequality is obtained similarly to the estimate for
I_{4}
.

For
\displaystyle I_{6}=-\varepsilon\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\operatorname{div}(\rho\mathbf{u})\frac{\triangle\rho}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}s
and
\displaystyle I_{7}=-\varepsilon\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\triangle\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\triangle\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}s
, similarly to
I_{4}
, there holds

(3.19)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}-\varepsilon\frac{\operatorname{div}(\rho\mathbf{u})\triangle\rho}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant\frac{\varepsilon^{r}}{3}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\mathbf{u}\right\|^{2}_{H^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]+C\varepsilon^{\frac{r}{2}},

where
C
depends on
\delta,\eta,\kappa,r,t
and
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{k_{2}r}\right]
, in which
k_{2}
is a specific positive constant.

(3.20)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(-\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\triangle\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\triangle\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant\frac{\varepsilon^{r}}{3}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\triangle\mathbf{u}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]+C\varepsilon^{\frac{r}{2}},

where
C
depends on
\delta,\eta,\kappa,r,t
and
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{k_{3}r}\right]
, in which
k_{3}
is a specific positive constant.

About
\displaystyle I_{8}=-r_{0}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\mathbf{u}|^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\right)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s
, it holds

\displaystyle\left|-r_{0}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\mathbf{u}|^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right|\leqslant r_{0}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho^{\frac{3}{4}}|\mathbf{u}|^{3}\left|\frac{\nabla\rho}{\rho\rho^{\frac{3}{4}}}\right|\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

(3.21)

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle r_{0}\left\|\rho^{-1-\frac{3}{4}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{\infty}}\left\|\rho^{\frac{3}{4}}|\mathbf{u}|^{3}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\frac{4}{3}}L_{x}^{\frac{4}{3}}}\left\|\nabla\rho\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle r_{0}\left\|\rho^{-1}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{\infty}}^{\frac{7}{4}}\left\|\rho^{\frac{3}{4}}|\mathbf{u}|^{3}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\frac{4}{3}}L_{x}^{\frac{4}{3}}}\left\|\nabla\triangle^{4}\rho\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{2}}.

Therefore, there holds

(3.22)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|-r_{0}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\mathbf{u}|^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right|^{r}\right]\leqslant C_{4},

where
C_{4}
depends on
r,t,r_{0},r_{1},\delta,\eta
and
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{k_{4}r}\right]
, in which
k_{4}
is a specific positive constant. This requires the terms
\eta\nabla\rho^{-10}
and
\delta\nabla\triangle^{9}\rho
to be left after
r_{0}|\mathbf{u}|^{2}\mathbf{u}
vanishes.

As for
\displaystyle I_{9}=-r_{1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho|\mathbf{u}|^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}s
, estimating

(3.23)

\displaystyle\left|-r_{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho|\mathbf{u}|^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right|=\left|-r_{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\mathbf{u}|^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\rho\operatorname{d}x\right|

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}r_{1}\rho|\mathbf{u}|^{2}|\nabla\mathbf{u}|\operatorname{d}x\leqslant C\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}r_{1}\rho|\mathbf{u}|^{4}\operatorname{d}x+\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho|\nabla\mathbf{u}|^{2}\operatorname{d}x,

\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho|\nabla\mathbf{u}|^{2}\operatorname{d}x
is controlled by
\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho|\mathbb{D}\mathbf{u}|^{2}\operatorname{d}x
, by the energy estimate (
2.3
), we have

(3.24)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{0}^{t}-r_{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho|\mathbf{u}|^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right|^{r}\right]\leqslant C(\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right]+1),

where
C
depends on
r,t
and
\sum\limits_{k=1}^{+\infty}f^{2}_{k}
.

About
\displaystyle I_{10}=-r_{2}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\right)\operatorname{d}s
, we calculate

(3.25)

-r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x=-r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\rho}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x=r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}(\ln\rho)_{t}\operatorname{d}x-\varepsilon r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\triangle\rho}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x.

We denote
\ln_{+}\rho=\ln\max\{\rho,1\}
,
\ln_{-}\rho=-\ln\min\{\rho,1\}
, therefore we have

(3.26)

\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\ln_{+}\rho\operatorname{d}x\leqslant\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho\mathrm{I}_{\{\rho>1\}}\operatorname{d}x\leqslant\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho^{\gamma}\mathrm{I}_{\{\rho>1\}}\operatorname{d}x\leqslant\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho^{\gamma}\operatorname{d}x.

So we have

(3.27)

\displaystyle\quad-r_{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\ln\rho\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle\leqslant r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\ln_{+}\rho-\ln_{-}\rho\right)(t)\operatorname{d}x+r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\ln_{-}\rho_{0}-\ln_{+}\rho_{0}\right)\operatorname{d}x+\varepsilon r_{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\left|\triangle\rho\right|}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle\leqslant r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\ln_{+}\rho(t)\operatorname{d}x-r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\ln_{-}\rho(t)\operatorname{d}x+r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\ln_{-}\rho_{0}\operatorname{d}x+\varepsilon r_{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\left|\triangle\rho\right|}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle\leqslant r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho^{\gamma}(t)\operatorname{d}x-r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\ln_{-}\rho(t)\operatorname{d}x+r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\ln_{-}\rho_{0}\operatorname{d}x+\varepsilon r_{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\left|\triangle\rho\right|}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s.

We move the term
\displaystyle-r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\ln_{-}\rho\operatorname{d}x
to the left-hand side. Next we take the expectation of its
r
-th power

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left|r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho^{\gamma}\operatorname{d}x+r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\ln_{-}\rho\operatorname{d}x+\varepsilon r_{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\left|\triangle\rho\right|}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right|^{r}\right]

(3.28)

\displaystyle\leqslant r_{2}C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho^{\gamma}\operatorname{d}x\right|^{r}\right]+r_{2}C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\ln_{-}\rho_{0}\operatorname{d}x\right|^{r}\right]+C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\varepsilon r_{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\left|\triangle\rho\right|}{\rho}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right|^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{1}+C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|r_{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\ln_{-}\rho_{0}\operatorname{d}x\right|^{r}\right]+C_{2}\varepsilon^{\frac{r}{2}},

where
C_{1}
depends on
r,r_{2},t
and
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right]
, and
C_{2}
depends on
r,r_{2},t,\delta,\eta
and
\mathbb{E}[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}]
.
Finally, with recalling to the definition (
3.1
), we obtain the stochastic B-D entropy:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{e}_{\rm reg}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\varepsilon\frac{4a}{\gamma}\left|\nabla\rho_{\rm reg}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right|^{2}+\varepsilon\eta\frac{11}{25}\left|\nabla\rho_{\rm reg}^{-5}\right|^{2}+\varepsilon\delta\left|\triangle^{5}\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}+\varepsilon\kappa\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\nabla^{2}\ln\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\left.\left.\left.+\frac{\rho_{\rm reg}}{2}\left|\nabla\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}-\nabla\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}^{\top}\right|^{2}+\varepsilon\left|\triangle\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}+r_{0}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|^{4}+r_{1}\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|^{4}+r_{2}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\left.\left.\left.+\eta\frac{11}{25}\left|\nabla\rho_{\rm reg}^{-5}\right|^{2}+\gamma\rho_{\rm reg}^{\gamma}\left|\nabla\ln\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}+\delta\left|\triangle^{5}\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}+\kappa\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\nabla^{2}\ln\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\right)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]

(3.29)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{3}\varepsilon^{\frac{r}{2}}+C_{4}+C_{5}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right]+1\right)+C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\nabla\ln\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]+\varepsilon^{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right\|^{2}_{H^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]

with recovered notations, where
C_{3}
depends on
\delta,\eta,\kappa,r,t
,
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right]
,
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{\frac{6r}{5}}\right]
,

\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{k_{1}r}\right]
,
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{k_{2}r}\right]
, and
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{k_{3}r}\right])
,
C_{4}
depends on
r,t,r_{0},r_{1},\eta,\delta
and
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{k_{4}r}\right]
,
C_{5}
depends on
r,t
and
\sum\limits_{k=1}^{+\infty}f^{2}_{k}
.

This proves that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{e}_{\rm reg}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\varepsilon\frac{4a}{\gamma}\left|\nabla\rho_{\rm reg}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right|^{2}+\varepsilon\eta\frac{11}{25}\left|\nabla\rho_{\rm reg}^{-5}\right|^{2}+\varepsilon\delta\left|\triangle^{5}\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}+\varepsilon\kappa\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\nabla^{2}\ln\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\right.\right.\right.

(3.30)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\qquad\qquad\left.\left.\left.+\frac{\rho_{\rm reg}}{2}\left|\nabla\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}-\nabla\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}^{\top}\right|^{2}+r_{0}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|^{4}+r_{1}\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|^{4}+r_{2}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\left.\left.\left.+\eta\frac{11}{25}\left|\nabla\rho_{\rm reg}^{-5}\right|^{2}+\gamma\rho_{\rm reg}^{\gamma}\left|\nabla\ln\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}+\delta\left|\triangle^{5}\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}+\kappa\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\nabla^{2}\ln\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\right)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{3}\varepsilon^{\frac{r}{2}}+C_{4}+C_{5}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right]+1\right)+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{\rm reg}\left|\nabla\ln\rho_{\rm reg}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]+C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right].

Therefore, by the definition of
\tilde{e}_{\rm reg}(t)
, we have

(3.31)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{\rm reg}(t)^{r}\right]\leqslant

\displaystyle C_{3}\varepsilon^{\frac{r}{2}}+C_{4}+C_{5}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right]+1\right)+C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right]+\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{\rm reg}(s)^{r}\right]\operatorname{d}s.

3.2.
Passing to the limit
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0

Step 1:
Choose the path space of the density and the velocity.

From (
3.1
), if we assume that
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right]
are bounded,
recalling lemma
2.2
for
\rho_{\rm reg}(x)
, it holds

(3.32)

\mathbb{E}\left[\kappa^{r}\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}H_{x}^{2}}^{2r}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\kappa^{r}\left\|\nabla\left(\rho_{\rm reg}^{\frac{1}{4}}\right)\right\|_{L_{t}^{4}L_{x}^{4}}^{4r}\right]\leqslant C,\quad r>4,

uniformly in
\varepsilon,\delta,\eta,\kappa
. The following estimates hold independent of
\varepsilon
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\eta^{r}\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}^{-1}\right\|^{10r}_{L_{x}^{10}}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{r}\left\|\nabla\sqrt{\rho_{\rm reg}}\right\|^{2r}_{L_{x}^{2}}\right]\leqslant C,

(3.33)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\delta^{r}\left\|\nabla\triangle^{4}\rho_{\rm reg}\right\|^{2r}_{L_{x}^{2}}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\eta^{r}\left\|\nabla\rho_{\rm reg}^{-5}\right\|^{2r}_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{2}}\right]\leqslant C,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\gamma^{r}\left\|\nabla\rho_{\rm reg}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2r}_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{2}}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\delta^{r}\left\|\triangle^{5}\rho_{\rm reg}\right\|^{2r}_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{2}}\right]\leqslant C,

where
r>4
and
C
depends on
\delta,\eta,\kappa,r,T,\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right],\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{\frac{6r}{5}}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{k_{{\rm I}}r}\right]
,
i=1,2,3
,
r>4
.
Similarly as in the last section, we give the following estimates from the mass equation without proof here:

(3.34)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\left(\rho_{\rm reg}\right)_{t}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{2}}^{r}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{2}}^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

(3.35)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\left(\rho_{\rm reg}\right)_{t}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}}^{r}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\left(\rho_{\rm reg}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)_{t}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{2}}^{r}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\rho_{\rm reg}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}H_{x}^{2}}^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

where
C
depends on
\delta,\eta,\kappa,r,t,\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{r}\right],\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{\frac{6r}{5}}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{\rm reg}(0)^{k_{{\rm I}}r}\right]
,
r>4
. Note that (
3.35
) will be independent on
\delta
and
\eta
after
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
.

Next, we choose the path space

(3.36)

\mathcal{X}_{3}=\mathcal{X}_{\rho_{0}}\times\mathcal{X}_{\mathbf{q}_{0}}^{3}\times\mathcal{X}_{\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0}}}}^{3}\times\mathcal{X}_{\rho}^{3}\times\mathcal{X}_{\mathbf{u}}^{3}\times\mathcal{X}_{\rho\mathbf{u}}^{3}\times\mathcal{X}_{W}^{3},\\

where
\mathcal{X}_{\rho_{0}}^{3}=L^{\gamma}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\cap L^{1}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\cap L^{-10}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\cap H^{9}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,
\mathcal{X}_{\mathbf{q}_{0}}^{3}=L^{1}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right))
,

\mathcal{X}_{\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0}}}}^{3}=L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,

\mathcal{X}_{\rho}^{3}=L^{2}\left([0,T];H^{10}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\cap L^{2}\left([0,T];W^{1,3}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\cap L^{\frac{5}{3}\gamma}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\cap C\left([0,T];W^{1,3}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
,

\mathcal{X}_{\mathbf{u}}^{3}=L^{2}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\cap L^{4}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,

\mathcal{X}_{\rho\mathbf{u}}^{3}=L^{2}\left([0,T];W^{1,\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\cap C\left([0,T];L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
,

\mathcal{X}_{W}^{3}=C\left([0,T];\mathcal{H}\right)
.

Step 2:
The tightness of the laws and the limit
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
.

Similarly as in last section, we have the following proposition and skip a proof here.

Proposition 3.1
.

\left\{\mathcal{L}\left[\rho_{0,{\rm reg}},\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm reg}},\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm reg}}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0,{\rm reg}}}},\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg},\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg},W_{\rm reg}\right]\right\}
is tight on
\mathcal{X}_{3}
.

Proposition 3.2
.

There exist two families of
\mathcal{X}_{3}
-valued Borel measurable random variables

\left\{\bar{\rho}_{0,{\rm reg}},\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,{\rm reg}},\frac{\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,{\rm reg}}}{\sqrt{\bar{\rho}_{0,{\rm reg}}}},\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg},\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg},\bar{W}_{\rm reg}\right\},

and
\left\{\rho_{0,{\rm I}},\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm I}},\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm I}}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0,{\rm I}}}},\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}},\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}},W\right\}
, defined on a new complete probability space, we still denote it as
\left(\bar{\Omega},\bar{\mathcal{F}},\bar{\mathbb{P}}\right)
, for convenience we still use the same notation in the following sections, such that (up to a subsequence):

(1)

\mathcal{L}\left[\bar{\rho}_{0,{\rm reg}},\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,{\rm reg}},\frac{\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,{\rm reg}}}{\sqrt{\bar{\rho}_{0,{\rm reg}}}},\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg},\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg},\bar{W}_{\rm reg}\right],

coincides with

\mathcal{L}\left[\rho_{0,{\rm reg}},\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm reg}},\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm reg}}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0,{\rm reg}}}},\rho_{\rm reg},\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg},\rho_{\rm reg}\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg},W_{\rm reg}\right]
on
\mathcal{X}_{3}
;

(2)

\mathcal{L}\left[\rho_{0,{\rm I}},\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm I}},\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm I}}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0,{\rm I}}}},\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}},\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}},W_{{\rm I}}\right]
on
\mathcal{X}_{3}
is a Radon measure;

(3)

The family of random variables
\left\{\bar{\rho}_{0,{\rm reg}},\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,{\rm reg}},\frac{\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,{\rm reg}}}{\sqrt{\bar{\rho}_{0,{\rm reg}}}},\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg},\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg},\bar{W}_{\rm reg}\right\}

converges to

\left\{\rho_{0,{\rm I}},\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm I}},\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm I}}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0,{\rm I}}}},\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}},\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}},W_{{\rm I}}\right\}
in the topology of
\mathcal{X}_{3}
,
\bar{\mathbb{P}}

a.s.
as
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
.

Step 3:
The system after taking the limit.

Similarly as the estimates in Lemma
2.3
, by Aubin-Lion’s lemma, we have the strong convergence of
\rho_{\rm reg}
:

(3.37)

\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}\rightarrow\rho_{{\rm I}}\quad\text{in}\quad L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{2}\left([0,T];W^{1,3}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right),\quad r>4;

uniformly in
\varepsilon
,
\eta
and
\delta
.
And similarly there holds the strong convergence of
\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}^{\frac{1}{2}}
:

(3.38)

\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}^{\frac{1}{2}}\rightarrow\rho_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\quad\text{in}\quad L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{2}\left([0,T];W^{1,6}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right),\quad r>4,

uniformly in
\varepsilon
,
\eta
and
\delta
.
W^{1,6}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\hookrightarrow C^{0,\frac{1}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,
\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg}\in L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{\infty}\left([0,T];L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
. So
\{\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg}\}
converges weakly to
\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}
in
L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{2}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
,
r>4
.

For this
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
layer, by the strong convergence of
\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}
and
\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg}
,
we have the almost everywhere convergence of
\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}
and
\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg}
, according to Vitali’s convergence theorem,

\rho_{{\rm I}}
satisfies the new equation

(3.39)

\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\right)_{t}+\operatorname{div}(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})=0,

in distribution
\bar{\mathbb{P}}
a.s.

Proposition 3.3
.

\left(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right)
satisfies the new system involving
\eta,\delta,\kappa
:

(3.40)

\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\right)_{t}+\operatorname{div}(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})=0,\\
\operatorname{d}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right)+\left(\operatorname{div}(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\otimes\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})+\nabla\left(a\rho_{{\rm I}}^{\gamma}\right)-\operatorname{div}(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbb{D}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\right)\operatorname{d}t\\
=\left(-r_{0}|\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}-r_{1}\rho_{{\rm I}}|\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}-r_{2}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}t+\left(\delta\rho_{{\rm I}}\nabla\triangle^{9}\rho_{{\rm I}}+\frac{11}{10}\eta\nabla\rho_{{\rm I}}^{-10}\right)\operatorname{d}t\\
\quad+\kappa\rho_{{\rm I}}\left(\nabla\left(\frac{\triangle\sqrt{\rho_{{\rm I}}}}{\sqrt{\rho_{{\rm I}}}}\right)\right)\operatorname{d}t+\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}W_{{\rm I}}\end{array}\right.

in distribution
\bar{\mathbb{P}}

a.s.

Proof.

For all
\varphi(t)\in C_{c}^{\infty}\left([0,T)\right)
, and all
\psi(x)\in C^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
, we have

(3.41)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\varepsilon\triangle\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}\varphi(t)\psi(x)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t\leqslant\varepsilon\delta^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\sqrt{\delta}\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}H_{x}^{10}}^{r}\left\|\varphi(t)\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}}\left\|\psi(x)\right\|_{H_{x}^{9}}\rightarrow 0,

as
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
,
\bar{\mathbb{P}}
a.s.
Correspondingly, we get

(3.42)

\displaystyle\quad\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\varepsilon\nabla\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}\nabla\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg}\varphi(t)\psi(x)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle\leqslant\varepsilon 2\left\|\nabla\sqrt{\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}}\right\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}L_{x}^{2}}\left\|\sqrt{\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}}\nabla\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{2}}\left\|\varphi(t)\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}}\left\|\psi(x)\right\|_{H_{x}^{9}}\rightarrow 0

as
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
,
\bar{\mathbb{P}}
a.s., and we have

(3.43)

\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\varepsilon\triangle^{2}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg}\varphi(t)\psi(x)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t\leqslant\varepsilon r_{0}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|r_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{2}}\left\|\varphi(t)\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}}\left\|\psi(x)\right\|_{H_{x}^{9}}\rightarrow 0,

as
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
. The weak convergence of
\nabla\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}^{-5}
,
\triangle^{5}\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}
,
\nabla\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}
,

\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}\nabla^{2}\ln\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}
still hold in the corresponding spaces. Next, we check the convergence of the term
associated with stochastic forces in the momentum equation. Since
\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg}\rightarrow\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}

strongly in
L^{2}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
uniformly in
\varepsilon,\eta,\delta
and
\bar{\mathbb{P}}
a.s.,
\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}\rightarrow\rho_{{\rm I}}
strongly in
L^{2}\left([0,T];W^{1,3}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)

uniformly in
\varepsilon,\eta,\delta
and
\bar{\mathbb{P}}
a.s., by (
1.9
), so we have the almost everywhere convergence
of
\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg}\right)
. Hence we still have the weak convergence of the stochastic term.
More precisely, we obtain

(3.44)

\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\bar{\rho}_{\rm reg},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{\rm reg}\right)\varphi(t)\psi(x)\operatorname{d}\bar{W}_{\rm reg}\operatorname{d}x\rightarrow\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{F}_{k}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right)\varphi(t)\psi(x)\operatorname{d}W_{{\rm I}}\operatorname{d}x

\bar{\mathbb{P}}
a.s.
\square

3.3.
Energy estimates for the approximated solutions after
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0

\mathbf{u}_{\rm reg}
satisfies the energy estimates (
3.46
) and (
3.1
). We also need to take the limit
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
to
get the new energy estimates of
\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}
.
By the lower semi-continuity of the integrals of convex
functions, we pass to the limit
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
, then (
3.2
) still holds. Let
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
in (
3.1
), then we gain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{{\rm I}}(t)^{r}\right]\leqslant

\displaystyle C_{4}+C_{5}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[e_{{\rm I}}(0)^{r}\right]+1\right)+C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{{\rm I}}(0)^{r}\right]+\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{{\rm I}}(s)^{r}\right]\operatorname{d}s

(3.45)

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C+C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{{\rm I}}(0)^{r}\right]+\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{{\rm I}}(s)^{r}\right]\operatorname{d}s.

C_{4}+C_{5}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[e_{{\rm I}}(0)^{r}\right]+1\right)+C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{{\rm I}}(0)^{r}\right]
is nondecreasing with
respect to
t
, by Grönwall’s inequality, we obtain

(3.46)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{{\rm I}}(t)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant\left(C+C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{{\rm I}}(0)^{r}\right]\right)e^{t}\leqslant C\left(1+\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{{\rm I}}(0)^{r}\right]\right),

where
C
depends on
\delta,\eta,r,t,r_{0},r_{1},\mathbb{E}\left[e_{{\rm I}}(0)^{r}\right],\mathbb{E}\left[e_{{\rm I}}(0)^{\frac{6r}{5}}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{{\rm I}}(0)^{k_{{\rm I}}r}\right]
,
i=1,2,3
,
4
. In conclusion, we write

(3.47)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\tilde{e}_{{\rm I}}(t)^{r}\right]\leqslant C\left(1+\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{{\rm I}}(0)^{r}\right]\right),r>4,

where
C
depends on
\delta,\eta,r,T,r_{0},r_{1},\mathbb{E}\left[e_{{\rm I}}(0)^{r}\right],\mathbb{E}\left[e_{{\rm I}}(0)^{\frac{6r}{5}}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[e_{{\rm I}}(0)^{k_{{\rm I}}r}\right]
,
i=1,2,3,4
.
Thereupon, we deduce

(3.48)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\frac{\rho_{{\rm I}}}{2}\left|\nabla\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}-\nabla\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}^{\top}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(r_{0}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{4}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

(3.49)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(r_{1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{{\rm I}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{4}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,\mathbb{E}\left[\left(r_{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

(3.50)

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\eta\frac{11}{25}\left|\nabla\rho_{{\rm I}}^{-5}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\gamma\rho_{{\rm I}}^{\gamma}\left|\nabla\ln\rho_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

(3.51)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\delta\left|\triangle^{5}\rho_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\kappa\rho_{{\rm I}}\left|\nabla^{2}\ln\rho_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

where
C
depends on
r,T,\mathbb{E}\left[e_{{\rm I}}(0)^{r}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{e}_{{\rm I}}(0)^{r}\right]
,
r>4
.

4.
Global existence of martingale solutions

In this section, when we take
\kappa\rightarrow 0
, the high regularity of
\rho
will be lost, which will cause the lack of regularity of
\rho\mathbf{u}
. Our target turns to derive the Mellet-Vasseur
[
32
]
type inequality in the stochastic version, which guarantees the strong convergence of
\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{u}
.

4.1.
Approximation of Mellet-Vasseur type inequality

As in
[
38
]
, we define a
C^{\infty}
cut-off functions
\phi_{K}
:

(4.1)

\phi_{K}(\rho)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}1,\text{ for any }\rho<K,\\
0,\text{ for any }\rho>2K,\\
\end{array}\right.

where
K>0
is any real number,
\left|\phi_{K}^{\prime}(\rho)\right|\leqslant\frac{4}{K}
. For any
\rho>0
, there exists
C>0
such that

\left|\phi_{K}^{\prime}(\rho)\sqrt{\rho}\right|+\left|\frac{\phi_{K}(\rho)}{\sqrt{\rho}}\right|\leqslant C,

where
C
depends on
K
.
For any
\mathbf{u}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we set
\mathbf{v}=\phi_{K}(\rho)\mathbf{u}
. Define
\varphi_{n}(\mathbf{u})=\tilde{\varphi}_{n}\left(|\mathbf{u}|^{2}\right)\in C^{1}\left(\mathds{R}^{3}\right)
,
where
\tilde{\varphi}_{n}
is given on
\mathds{R}^{+}
by

(4.2)

\tilde{\varphi}_{n}(y)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}(1+y)\ln(1+y),&0\leqslant y<n,\\
2(1+\ln(1+n))y-(1+y)\ln(1+y)+2(\ln(1+n)-n),&n\leqslant y\leqslant C_{n},\\
e(1+n)^{2}-2n-2,&y\geq C_{n},\end{array}\right.

with
\tilde{\varphi}_{n}(0)=0
, and
C_{n}=e(1+n)^{2}-1
. So we have

(4.3)

\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime}(y)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}1+\ln(1+y),&\text{ if }0\leqslant y\leqslant n,\\
1+2\ln(1+n)-\ln(1+y)\geq 0,&\text{ if }n<y\leqslant C_{n},\\
0,&\text{ if }y>C_{n}.\end{array}\right.

and
\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime}(y)\leqslant 1+\ln(1+n)
for
n<y\leqslant C_{n}
. And we have

(4.4)

\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime\prime}(y)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\frac{1}{1+y},&\text{ if }0\leqslant y\leqslant n,\\
-\frac{1}{1+y},&\text{ if }n<y\leqslant C_{n},\\
0,&\text{ if }y>C_{n}.\end{array}\right.

\tilde{\varphi}_{n}(y)
is a nondecreasing function with respect to
y
for any fixed
n
, and it is a nondecreasing function with respect to
n
for any fixed
y
. Further, the convergence holds:

(4.5)

\tilde{\varphi}_{n}(y)\rightarrow(1+y)\ln(1+y)\text{ almost everywhere as }n\rightarrow\infty.

We denote
\nabla_{\mathbf{u}}
as taking derivative with respect to
\mathbf{u}
, we have

(4.6)

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{u}}\varphi_{n}(\mathbf{u})=

\displaystyle 2\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime}\left(|\mathbf{u}|^{2}\right)\mathbf{u},

(4.7)

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{u}}^{2}\varphi_{n}(\mathbf{u})=

\displaystyle 2\left(2\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime\prime}\left(|\mathbf{u}|^{2}\right)\mathbf{u}\otimes\mathbf{u}+\mathbb{I}_{3}\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime}\left(|\mathbf{u}|^{2}\right)\right).

In last section, we know that
\rho_{{\rm I}}
,
\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}
satisfies (
3.40
) weakly
\bar{\mathbb{P}}
a.s.
We approximate (
3.40
) by

(4.8)

\operatorname{d}(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}})+\left(\operatorname{div}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\otimes\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)-\operatorname{div}\mathbb{S}_{{\rm I}}+\mathbf{R}_{{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}t=\phi_{K}(\rho_{{\rm I}})\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}W_{{\rm I}},

where

\displaystyle\mathbb{S}_{{\rm I}}=

\displaystyle\rho_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}(\rho_{{\rm I}})\left(\mathbb{D}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}+\kappa\frac{\Delta\sqrt{\rho_{{\rm I}}}}{\sqrt{\rho_{{\rm I}}}}\mathbb{I}_{3}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{{\rm I}}=

\displaystyle\rho_{{\rm I}}^{2}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}^{\prime}(\rho_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{div}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}+2\rho_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\nabla\rho_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\phi_{K}(\rho_{{\rm I}})+\rho_{{\rm I}}\nabla\phi_{K}(\rho_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{D}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}

(4.9)

\displaystyle+r_{0}|\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}(\rho_{{\rm I}})+r_{1}\rho_{{\rm I}}|\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}(\rho_{{\rm I}})+r_{2}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\right)

\displaystyle-\eta\frac{11}{10}\nabla\rho_{{\rm I}}^{-10}\phi_{K}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\right)-\delta\rho_{{\rm I}}\nabla\triangle^{9}\rho_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}(\rho_{{\rm I}})+\kappa\sqrt{\rho_{{\rm I}}}\nabla\phi_{K}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\right)\triangle\sqrt{\rho_{{\rm I}}}

\displaystyle+2\kappa\phi_{K}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\right)\nabla\sqrt{\rho_{{\rm I}}}\triangle\sqrt{\rho_{{\rm I}}}.

Let

\hbar(\rho_{{\rm I}},\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}})=\rho_{{\rm I}}\left(1+\left|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\right)\ln\left(1+\left|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\right)=\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}+\frac{\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}}{\rho_{{\rm I}}}\right)\ln\left(1+\frac{\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}}{\rho_{{\rm I}}^{2}}\right).

Since

(4.10)

\langle\operatorname{d}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right),\operatorname{d}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\rangle=\rho_{{\rm I}}^{2}\phi_{K}^{2}(\rho_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\otimes\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}t,

by Itô’s formula, we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{d}\hbar\left(\rho_{{\rm I}},\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)=

\displaystyle\hbar_{\rho_{{\rm I}}}\operatorname{d}\rho_{{\rm I}}+\nabla_{(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}})}\hbar\operatorname{d}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)+\frac{\nabla_{(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}})}^{2}\hbar}{2}\langle\operatorname{d}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right),\operatorname{d}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\left(1-|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)\ln\left(1+|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)-2|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)\operatorname{d}\rho_{{\rm I}}

\displaystyle+\left(2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\ln\left(1+|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)+2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)+\frac{\nabla_{(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}})}^{2}\hbar}{2}\langle\operatorname{d}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right),\operatorname{d}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\rangle

(4.11)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left(\left(1-|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)\ln\left(1+|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)-2|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)\operatorname{div}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle+\left(2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\ln\left(1+|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)+2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\left(-\operatorname{div}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\otimes\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\right)\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle+\left(2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\ln\left(1+|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)+2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\left(\operatorname{div}\mathbb{S}_{{\rm I}}-\mathbf{R}_{{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle+\left(2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\ln\left(1+|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)+2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}W_{{\rm I}}

\displaystyle+\rho_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}^{2}(\rho_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\left(\left(1+\ln\left(1+|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)\right)\mathbb{I}_{3}+2\frac{\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\otimes\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}}{\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}+|\rho_{{\rm I}}|^{2}}\right)\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}t.

To simplify (
4.1
), we compute
\operatorname{div}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\otimes\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)=\operatorname{div}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right)\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}+\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\cdot\nabla\right)\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}
.
Taking integration on
\mathbb{T}^{3}
with respect to
t
and
x
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\hbar\left(\rho_{{\rm I}},\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\left(-|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}-1\right)\ln\left(1+|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)\operatorname{div}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}s\operatorname{d}x

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\cdot\left(\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\cdot\nabla\right)\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}s\operatorname{d}x-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\ln\left(1+|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)\cdot\left(\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\cdot\nabla\right)\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}s\operatorname{d}x

(4.12)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\left(2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\ln\left(1+|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)+2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\left(\operatorname{div}\mathbb{S}_{{\rm I}}-\mathbf{R}_{{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}s\operatorname{d}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\left(2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\ln\left(1+|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)+2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\rho_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}(\rho_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}W_{{\rm I}}\operatorname{d}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\rho_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}^{2}(\rho_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\left(\left(1+\ln\left(1+|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)\right)\mathbb{I}_{3}+2\frac{\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\otimes\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}}{\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}+|\rho_{{\rm I}}|^{2}}\right)\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}s\operatorname{d}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\hbar\left(\rho_{0,{\rm I}},\rho_{0,{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{0,{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}x.

The first three integrals on the right-hand side will cancel. So we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\hbar\left(\rho_{{\rm I}},\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\ln|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}+2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\left(\operatorname{div}\mathbb{S}_{{\rm I}}-\mathbf{R}_{{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

(4.13)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\left(2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\ln|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}+2\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)\phi_{K}(\rho_{{\rm I}})\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}W_{{\rm I}}\operatorname{d}x

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}^{2}(\rho_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\left(\left(1+\ln\left(1+|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)\right)\mathbb{I}_{3}+2\frac{\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\otimes\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}}{\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}+|\rho_{{\rm I}}|^{2}}\right)\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\hbar\left(\rho_{0,{\rm I}},\rho_{0,{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{0,{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}x.

Similarly formula (
4.1
) holds for
|\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\geqslant n^{2}
, with setting

(4.14)

\displaystyle\hslash\left(\rho_{{\rm I}},\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\rho_{{\rm I}}\left(2\left(1+\ln(1+n)\right)\frac{\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}}{\rho_{{\rm I}}^{2}}-\left(1+\frac{\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}}{\rho_{{\rm I}}^{2}}\right)\ln\left(1+\frac{\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}}{\rho_{{\rm I}}^{2}}\right)+2\left(\ln(1+n)-n\right)\right).

In summary, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{{\rm I}}\varphi_{n}(\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}x=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}}\varphi_{n}(\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}})\mathbf{R}_{{\rm I}}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\mathbb{S}_{{\rm I}}:\nabla\left(\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}}\varphi_{n}(\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}})\right)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

(4.15)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}}\varphi_{n}(\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}})\rho_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}(\rho_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}W_{{\rm I}}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}^{2}(\rho_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}}^{2}\varphi_{n}(\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{0,{\rm I}}\varphi_{n}\left(\mathbf{v}_{0,{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}x.

4.2.
Vanishing the quantum effect:
\kappa\rightarrow 0

Step 1:
Choose the path space.

We choose the space
\mathcal{X}_{4}=\mathcal{X}_{\rho_{0}}^{4}\times\mathcal{X}_{\mathbf{q}_{0}}^{4}\times\mathcal{X}_{\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0}}}}^{4}\times\mathcal{X}_{\rho}^{4}\times\mathcal{X}_{\mathbf{u}}^{4}\times\mathcal{X}_{\rho\mathbf{u}}^{4}\times\mathcal{X}_{W}^{4}
,
where
\mathcal{X}_{\rho_{0}}^{4}=L^{\gamma}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,
\mathcal{X}_{\mathbf{q}_{0}}^{4}=L^{1}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,

\mathcal{X}_{\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0}}}}^{4}=L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,

\mathcal{X}_{\rho}^{4}=L^{2}\left(0,T;L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\cap L^{\frac{5}{3}\gamma}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,

\mathcal{X}_{\mathbf{u}}^{4}=L^{2}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\cap L^{4}\left([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)
,

\mathcal{X}_{\rho\mathbf{u}}^{4}

=C\left([0,T];L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
,
\mathcal{X}_{W}^{4}=C\left([0,T];\mathcal{H}\right)
.

Step 2:
The tightness of the laws.

We then consider the regularity of
\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}
and have the following tightness.

Proposition 4.1
.

The set
\left\{\mathcal{L}\left[\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right]\right\}
is tight on
\mathcal{X}_{\rho\mathbf{u}}^{4}=C\left([0,T];L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)
for
\gamma>1
.

Proof: We first consider the deterministic part, and we denote

(4.16)

\displaystyle Y_{{\rm I}}(t)=

\displaystyle\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}(0)-\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}(\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\otimes\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}\mathbb{S}_{{\rm I}}\operatorname{d}s-\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{R}_{{\rm I}}\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\rho_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}^{2}(\rho_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}}}^{2}\varphi_{n}(\mathbf{v}_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}s.

In this layer,
\kappa
goes to
0
later. The high regularity on
\rho_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}
and
\rho_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{4}}
will not hold uniformly on
\kappa
, but we use
\rho_{{\rm I}}^{\gamma}\in L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{1}\left([0,T];L^{3}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
,
\rho_{{\rm I}}\in L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{\infty}\left([0,T];L^{\gamma}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
uniformly in
\kappa
to derive that

\frac{\operatorname{d}Y_{{\rm I}}(t)}{\operatorname{d}t}
is bounded in
L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{\frac{4}{3}}\left([0,T];W^{-l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
for
l>\frac{5}{2}
uniformly in
\kappa,\eta,\delta
.
Similar as in Proposition
2.7
,

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|Y_{{\rm I}}(t)\right\|_{C^{0,\varsigma}\left([0,T];W^{-l,2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)}^{r}\right]\leqslant C
uniformly in
\kappa
, where
C
depends on
r
,
\varsigma<\frac{1}{4}
.
Next, we study the time regularity of stochastic integral.
Applying the Burkhölder-Davis-Gundy’s inequality and Hölder’s inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\phi_{K}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}}\right)\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbb{F}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}W_{{\rm I}}\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}\right)^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbb{F}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right)\right|\operatorname{d}W_{{\rm I}}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}\operatorname{d}x\right)^{\frac{2}{3}r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbb{F}\left(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right)\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}t\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}\operatorname{d}x\right)^{\frac{2}{3}r}\right]

(4.17)

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\sum\limits_{k=0}^{\infty}f_{k}^{2}\left(\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\right|+\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right|\right)^{2}\operatorname{d}t\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}\operatorname{d}x\right)^{\frac{2}{3}r}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\sum\limits_{k=0}^{\infty}f_{k}^{2}\right)^{\frac{r}{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\left(\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\right|+\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right|\right)^{2}\operatorname{d}t\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}\operatorname{d}x\right)^{\frac{2}{3}r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\left(\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}+\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\right)\operatorname{d}t\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}\operatorname{d}x\right)^{\frac{2}{3}r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\left(\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}+\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\right)\operatorname{d}t\operatorname{d}x\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\operatorname{d}x\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}r}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\left(\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}+\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\right)\operatorname{d}t\operatorname{d}x\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}r}\right],

where
C
depends on
r,\sum\limits_{k=0}^{\infty}f_{k}^{2}
. By the B-D entropy estimates, we have

\nabla\rho_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\in L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{\infty}\left([0,T];L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right),

by Sobolev’s embedding, we further have

(4.18)

\displaystyle\rho_{{\rm I}}\in L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{\infty}\left([0,T];L^{3}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right).

We estimate

(4.19)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}t\operatorname{d}x=

\displaystyle\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t\leqslant\left\|\rho_{{\rm I}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{3}}^{2}\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\operatorname{d}x\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}\operatorname{d}t

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle\left\|\rho_{{\rm I}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{3}}^{2}\left(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\right).

So we have

(4.20)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}t\operatorname{d}x\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}r}\right]\leqslant\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\rho_{{\rm I}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{3}}^{r}\right]\left(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\right)^{\frac{r}{2}}.

We employ the regularity of
r_{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{{\rm I}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{4}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}t\right)^{r}\right]\leqslant C
and the high regularity of
\rho
from the B-D entropy to estimate

(4.21)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\left|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}t\operatorname{d}x\leqslant\left\|\rho_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{2}}\left\|\rho_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle\left\|\rho_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{2}}\left\|\rho_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{2}}\left(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Thereupon, up to a modification it holds

(4.22)

\int_{0}^{t}\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbb{F}(\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}W_{{\rm I}}\in L^{r}\left(\Omega;C^{0,\varsigma}\left([0,T];L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right),

where
\varsigma<\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{r}
is a positive constant. We choose some
r>4
here and hereafter. The analysis of deterministic part
Y_{{\rm I}}(t)
and (
4.22
) imply that
\left\|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)}
is continuous in
[0,T]
uniformly in
\kappa
,
\eta
,
\delta
and
r_{0}
, up to a modification, i.e.,

(4.23)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\right\|_{C^{0,\varsigma}\left([0,T];L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)}^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

where C depends on
r
and the initial data. With a standard argument as in previous section, we have the tightness of
\mathcal{L}\left[\{\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}}\}\right]

in
\mathcal{X}_{\rho\mathbf{u}}^{4}
.
\square

Step 3:
The convergence
\kappa\rightarrow 0
.

We have the corresponding tightness and Skorokhod’s representation theorem.

Proposition 4.2
.

There exists two families of
\mathcal{X}_{4}
-valued Borel measurable random variables,

\left\{\bar{\rho}_{0,{\rm I}},\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,{\rm I}},\frac{\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,{\rm I}}}{\sqrt{\bar{\rho}_{0,{\rm I}}}},\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}},\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}},\bar{W}_{{\rm I}}\right\},

and
\left\{\rho_{0,{\rm II}},\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm II}},\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0}}},\rho_{{\rm II}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}},\rho_{{\rm II}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}},W_{{\rm II}}\right\}
, defined on a new complete probability space

\left(\bar{\Omega},\bar{\mathcal{F}},\bar{\mathbb{P}}\right)
, such that (up to a subsequence):

(1)

\mathcal{L}\left[\bar{\rho}_{0,{\rm I}},\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,{\rm I}},\frac{\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,{\rm I}}}{\sqrt{\bar{\rho}_{0,{\rm I}}}},\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}},\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}},\bar{W}_{{\rm I}}\right],

coincides with

\mathcal{L}\left[\rho_{0,{\rm I}},\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm I}},\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm I}}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0,{\rm I}}}},\rho_{{\rm I}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}},\rho_{{\rm I}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm I}},W_{{\rm I}}\right]
on
\mathcal{X}_{4}
;

(2)

\mathcal{L}\left[\rho_{0,{\rm II}},\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm II}},\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm II}}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0,{\rm II}}}},\rho_{{\rm II}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}},\rho_{{\rm II}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}},W_{{\rm II}}\right]
on
\mathcal{X}_{4}
is a Radon measure;

(3)

The family of random variables
\left\{\bar{\rho}_{0,{\rm I}},\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,{\rm I}},\frac{\bar{\mathbf{q}}_{0,{\rm I}}}{\sqrt{\bar{\rho}_{0,{\rm I}}}},\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}},\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}},\bar{W}_{{\rm I}}\right\}

converges to

\left\{\rho_{0,{\rm II}},\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm II}},\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm II}}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0,{\rm II}}}},\rho_{{\rm II}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}},\rho_{{\rm II}}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}},W_{{\rm II}}\right\}
in the topology of
\mathcal{X}_{4}
,
\bar{\mathbb{P}}

a.s.
as
\kappa\rightarrow 0
.

Step 4:
The estimates after taking the limit.

Corresponding to
[
38
]
, we handle the deterministic terms by taking
K=\kappa^{-\frac{3}{4}}
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}x\right|^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}})\mathbf{R}_{{\rm I}}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\mathbb{S}_{{\rm I}}:\nabla\left(\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}})\right)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right|^{r}\right]

(4.24)

\displaystyle+C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}})\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\mathbb{F}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}W_{{\rm I}}\right|^{r}\right]

\displaystyle+C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}^{2}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{F}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}})\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}^{2}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{F}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right|^{r}\right]

\displaystyle+C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{0,{\rm I}}\varphi_{n}\left(\frac{\mathbf{q}_{0,{\rm I}}}{\rho_{0,{\rm I}}}\right)\operatorname{d}x\right|^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C,

where
C
depends on
\mathbb{E}[(e_{{\rm I}}(0))^{r}],r,t\text{ and }n
,
C_{r}
is a constant merely dependent of
r
.
We study the convergence of
\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}
.

(4.25)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\operatorname{div}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}}^{r}\right]\leqslant\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{6}}^{r}\left\|\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\operatorname{div}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{2}}^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

together with

(4.26)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right)\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{1}}^{r}\right]\leqslant\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{2}}^{r}\left\|\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{4}}^{r}\left\|\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{4}L_{x}^{4}}^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

implies

(4.27)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\left(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\right)_{t}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{1}}^{r}\right]\leqslant C,

uniformly in
\delta,\eta
, here
C
depends on
r,r_{1}
,
r>4
.

We calculate that

(4.28)

|\nabla\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}|=2\left|\nabla\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right|\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}},\\

so
\{\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\}
is bounded in
L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{\infty}\left(0,T;W^{1,1}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
. By Aubin-Lion’s lemma we obtain the strong convergence of
\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}
in
L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{2}\left(0,T;L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
.

From the stochastic B-D entropy (
3.47
), we know

(4.29)

-r_{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\ln_{-}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\operatorname{d}x\right|^{r}\right]\leqslant C,\text{ for some }r>4,

where
C
depends on
r,T,\mathbb{E}[e_{{\rm I}}(0)]^{r},\mathbb{E}[\tilde{e}_{{\rm I}}(0)]^{r})
.
Vassuer-Yu
[
38
]
argued that

(4.30)

\int_{\Omega}\left(\ln\left(\frac{1}{\rho_{{\rm II}}}\right)\right)_{+}dx\leq\int_{\Omega}\lim\inf_{\kappa\rightarrow 0}\left(\ln\left(\frac{1}{\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}}\right)\right)_{+}dx\leq\lim\inf_{\kappa\rightarrow 0}\int_{\Omega}\left(\ln\left(\frac{1}{\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}}\right)\right)_{+}dx,

by Fatou’s lemma, so
\left(\ln\left(\frac{1}{\rho_{{\rm II}}}\right)\right)_{+}
is bounded in
L^{r}\left(\Omega;L^{\infty}\left(0,T;L^{1}\left(\mathbb{T}^{3}\right)\right)\right)
. Therefore (
4.29
) allows us to conclude that

(4.31)

\left|\left\{x\mid\rho_{{\rm II}}(t,x)=0\right\}\right|=0,\quad\text{ for almost every }t,x,\omega.

For almost every
(t,x)
,
\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}(t,x)\neq 0
, the convergence

\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}(t,x)=\frac{\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}}{\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}}\rightarrow\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}}(t,x)

and

\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}\rightarrow\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}}(t,x)

hold
\mathbb{P}
a.s. as
\kappa\rightarrow 0
.
\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\rightarrow 1
holds almost everywhere as
K\rightarrow\infty
,
\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}\varphi_{n}\leqslant 1+\ln(1+C_{n})
uniformly in
K
for fixed
n
, therefore
\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}})\rightarrow\nabla_{\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}}}\varphi_{n}\left(\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}}\right)
holds almost everywhere.
r_{0}|\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})+\eta\nabla\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{-10}\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})+\delta\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\nabla\triangle^{9}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\rightarrow r_{0}|\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}}|^{2}\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}}+\eta\nabla\rho_{{\rm II}}^{-10}+\delta\rho_{{\rm II}}\nabla\triangle^{9}\rho_{{\rm II}}
holds almost everywhere. These three terms are
L^{1}
integrable. By Lebesgue’s dominated convergence theorem, taking the limit
K\rightarrow\infty
, i.e.
\kappa\rightarrow 0
, we have

(4.32)

\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\varphi_{n}(\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}x\rightarrow\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{{\rm II}}\varphi_{n}(\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}})\operatorname{d}x\quad\bar{\mathbb{P}}\text{ a.s.}

For the pressure term, integration by parts implies

(4.33)

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}})\nabla\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\gamma}\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}^{2}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}):\nabla\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\gamma}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}})\nabla\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\gamma}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\triangleq P_{1}+P_{2},

where

(4.34)

\displaystyle P_{1}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\gamma}\phi_{K}^{2}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}^{2}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}):\nabla\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\gamma}\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}^{2}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}):\nabla\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\otimes\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\triangleq P_{11}+P_{12}.

Due to
\phi_{K}^{\prime}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leqslant\frac{4}{K^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, we have

(4.35)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[|P_{2}|^{r}\right]\leqslant

\displaystyle C\mathbb{E}\left[\kappa^{-\frac{1}{4}}\left\|\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi_{K}^{\prime}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{\infty}}^{r}\left\|\kappa^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{4}L_{x}^{4}}^{r}\left\|\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\gamma+\frac{1}{4}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\frac{4}{3}}L_{x}^{\frac{4}{3}}}^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C\left(\kappa^{-\frac{1}{4}}\frac{2}{K^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)^{r}=C\kappa^{\frac{1}{8}r}\rightarrow 0\text{ as }\kappa\rightarrow 0,

where
C
depends on
\mathbb{E}[(e_{{\rm I}}(0))^{r}],r,t\text{ and }n
. We estimate

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[|P_{12}|^{r}\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\gamma+\frac{1}{4}}\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}^{2}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}):\phi_{K}^{\prime}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})2\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\operatorname{d}s\operatorname{d}x\right|^{r}\right]

(4.36)

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C\mathbb{E}\left[\kappa^{-\frac{1}{4}}\left\|\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi_{K}^{\prime}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\right\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L_{x}^{\infty}}^{r}\left\|\kappa^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{4}L_{x}^{4}}^{r}\left\|\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\gamma+\frac{1}{4}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{\frac{4}{3}}L_{x}^{\frac{4}{3}}}^{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C\left(\kappa^{-\frac{1}{4}}\frac{2}{K^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)^{r}=C\kappa^{\frac{1}{8}r}\rightarrow 0\text{ as }\kappa\rightarrow 0,

in which we used
\left|\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}^{2}\varphi_{n}\mathbf{v}_{k}\right|\leqslant C(n)
, with
C
depending on
\mathbb{E}[(e_{{\rm I}}(0))^{r}],r,t\text{ and }n
.
Since

(4.37)

\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}^{2}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}):\nabla\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}=4\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime\prime}\left(|\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\right)\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}\otimes\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}:\nabla\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}+2\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime}\left(\left|\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\right)\operatorname{div}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}},

we have

\displaystyle P_{11}=4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\phi_{K}^{2}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\gamma}\left|\nabla\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right|\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s+2\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\phi_{K}^{2}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\gamma}\left|\operatorname{div}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right|\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime}\left(\left|\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\right)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

(4.38)

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle 4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\left|\nabla\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}+4\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{2\gamma-1}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s+2\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\phi_{K}^{2}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\gamma}\left|\mathbb{D}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right|\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime}(\left|\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2})\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C+2\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\phi_{K}^{4}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\left|\mathbb{D}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime}\left(\left|\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\right)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s+2\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime}\left(\left|\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\right)\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{2\gamma-1}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s,

with
C
depending on
\mathbb{E}[(e_{{\rm I}}(0))^{r}],r,t\text{ and }n
.

(4.39)

\displaystyle\mathbb{S}_{{\rm I}}=\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\left(\mathbb{D}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}+\kappa\frac{\Delta\sqrt{\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}}}{\sqrt{\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}}}\mathbb{I}_{3}\right)\triangleq\mathbb{S}_{1}+\mathbb{S}_{2},

We estimate term by term,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}):\mathbb{S}_{1}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla\left(\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}})\right):\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{D}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

(4.40)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}^{2}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}})\nabla\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}:\mathbb{D}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}^{\top}\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}^{2}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{D}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\nabla\left(\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\right)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle A_{1}+A_{2}.

Similarly,
\mathbb{E}\left[|A_{2}|^{r}\right]\rightarrow 0
as
\kappa\rightarrow 0
.

\displaystyle A_{1}=

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\phi_{K}^{2}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime}(|\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}|^{2})\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\nabla\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}:\mathbb{D}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\phi_{K}^{4}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime}(|\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}|^{2})\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\left|\mathbb{D}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}s\operatorname{d}x-4\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\phi_{K}^{2}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\left|\mathbb{D}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s,

combining with the pressure term
P_{11}
, it holds

(4.42)

\displaystyle-A_{1}+P_{11}\leqslant

\displaystyle(-2+2)\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\phi_{K}^{4}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime}(|\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}|^{2})\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\left|\mathbb{D}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle+4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\phi_{K}^{2}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\left|\mathbb{D}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s+2\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime}\left(\left|\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\right)\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{2\gamma-1}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s,

and

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|-A_{1}+P_{11}\right|^{r}\right]\leqslant C+C_{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|2\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\varphi}_{n}^{\prime}\left(\left|\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}\right|^{2}\right)\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{2\gamma-1}\operatorname{d}s\operatorname{d}x\right|^{r}\right],

. We also have

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle C\kappa\kappa^{-\frac{1}{4}}\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\kappa^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{4}L_{x}^{4}}^{r}\left\|\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\triangle\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}L_{x}^{2}}^{r}\left\|\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\right\|_{L_{t}^{4}L_{x}^{4}}^{r}\leqslant C\kappa^{\frac{1}{4}}\text{ as }\kappa\rightarrow 0,

\mathbb{E}[(e_{{\rm I}}(0))^{r}],r,t\text{ and }n
.
Similarly, there holds

(4.45)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}})\mathbb{S}_{2}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\right|^{r}\right]\rightarrow 0;

(4.46)

\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}})\left(r_{0}|\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}+r_{1}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}|\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}|^{2}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}+r_{2}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right)\phi_{K}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{d}s\operatorname{d}x\geqslant 0;

and

(4.47)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}^{2}\left(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}\right)\mathbb{F}\left(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right)\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}^{2}\varphi_{n}\left(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}\right)\mathbb{F}\left(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\right)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\rho_{{\rm II}}\mathbb{F}\left(\rho_{{\rm II}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}}\right)\nabla_{\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}}}^{2}\varphi_{n}\left(\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}}\right)\mathbb{F}\left(\rho_{{\rm II}},\mathbf{u}_{{\rm II}}\right)\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s\text{ as }K\rightarrow\infty,

\bar{\mathbb{P}}
a.s. according to Lebesgue’s dominated convergence theorem. The terms

(4.48)

-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\nabla_{\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}}}^{2}\varphi_{n}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{{\rm I}})\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{2}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\phi_{K}^{\prime}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\operatorname{div}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{{\rm I}}\operatorname{d}x\operatorname{d}s,

and

will go to zero due to
\phi_{K}^{\prime}(\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}})\bar{\rho}_{{\rm I}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leqslant\frac{4}{K^{\frac{1}{2}}}
.
Lemma
2.1
implies that
